An error taxonomy separates surface errors (numerals, punctuation) from structural ones (conjuncts, matras, nukta), and a byte-level (ByT5) post-corrector improves a cheap engine on its own error distribution (chrF++ +1.2 to +1.5) but does not transfer across engines. We release the benchmark, code, and models.

</details>


### [112] [Understanding Evaluation Illusion in Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29228)
*Hengxiang Zhang,Jiaxi Ren,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 扩散大型语言模型的解码方法评估对提示模板高度敏感，单模板评估可能误导结论，并行解码方法未超越单令牌基线，作者提出可靠评估指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大型语言模型需要大量去噪步骤以维持生成质量，但现有解码方法评估结果在看似相同设置下不一致，风险导致有偏见结论，因此需理解评估问题。

Method: 作者通过跨多种评价设置的严格评估和全面实验，分析当前解码方法。

Result: 解码方法排名对提示模板选择高度敏感；单模板评估产生效率提升错觉；并行解码方法始终低于单令牌解码基线；有效提示模板可显著提升结果；其他被忽视的评价设置也影响评估。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者提出了可靠评估解码方法的实用指南。

Abstract: Despite the capability of parallel decoding, diffusion large language models (dLLMs) require many denoising steps to maintain generation quality, motivating recent research on efficient decoding strategies. However, existing studies have reported inconsistent evaluation results even under seemingly identical evaluation settings, risking biased conclusions about dLLM decoding methods. To understand this evaluation concern, we conduct a rigorous evaluation of current decoding methods for dLLMs across diverse evaluation settings. Surprisingly, our analysis reveals that the ranking of decoding methods is highly sensitive to the choice of prompt templates. Single-template evaluation can lead to an illusion that decoding methods improve inference efficiency without performance degradation. Through comprehensive experiments, we find that current parallel decoding methods consistently underperform the single-token decoding baseline, failing to overcome the speed-quality trade-off. We further identify this evaluation inconsistency as the high sensitivity of parallel decoding methods to minor variations in prompt templates. Our experiments show that an effective prompt template can achieve strong evaluation results even with fewer denoising steps, markedly outperforming the marginal gain from increasing denoising steps. Beyond prompt templates, our experiments indicate that overlooked evaluation settings can also notably affect the assessment of decoding methods. Based on these findings, we propose practical guidelines for the reliable evaluation of decoding methods in dLLMs.

</details>


### [113] [Travel-Oriented Reasoning Large Language Model via Domain-Specific Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29254)
*Vignesh Ram Nithin Kappagantula,Shayan Hassantabar,Samuel Simpson,Golnaz Moallem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于专家设计知识图谱的模块化管道，用于构建旅行领域的推理大语言模型。该方法通过监督微调将领域知识嵌入到具有推理能力的LLM中，在基准测试中取得了82.4%的精确匹配率，显著优于预训练基线模型的22.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具备广泛的推理能力，但在旅行等专业化领域中面临准确性和可靠性问题。这些领域需要精确的定义、规则和专家定义的概念框架。模型的问题不是缺少领域知识，而是没有内化底层领域图谱，导致产生自信但无根据的输出。

Method: 提出了一种模块化管道，包括：1) 旅行知识图谱（KG），编码领域实体及其关系；2) 自下而上的构建过程，遍历KG生成多跳问答对；3) 监督微调阶段，使用生成的问答对作为可审计的推理轨迹，将领域知识嵌入到具有推理能力的LLM中；4) 旅行领域基准数据集，用于测量微调模型的准确性和校准度。使用Qwen3-4B配合LoRA适配进行评估。

Result: 推理模型在基准测试中取得了82.4%的精确匹配率，显著优于预训练Qwen3-4B基线的22.4%。校准分析将剩余17.57%的错误分解为两种不同的失败模式：过度自信的多标签解码器在大多数双答案错误中预测正确答案加上一个虚假选项；以及较小的推理失败，在单答案问题中，支持事实存在于KG中，但模型未能重建正确的多跳路径。

Conclusion: 显式的基于KG的推理显著提高了LLM在专业化领域的准确性和不确定性解释能力，并将每选项校准和轨迹长度感知解码确定为下一个改进方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate broad reasoning abilities but struggle with accuracy and reliability in specialized domains such as travel, where reasoning depends on precise definitions, rules, and expert-defined conceptual frameworks, and where confident but unfounded outputs arise from a reasoning failure in which the model has not internalized the underlying domain graph rather than from missing domain knowledge alone. We propose a modular pipeline for building a travel-domain reasoning LLM grounded in an expert-designed knowledge graph (KG). Our pipeline integrates a travel KG that encodes domain entities and their relationships, a bottom-up construction procedure that walks the KG to produce multi-hop question answer (QA) pairs, a supervised fine-tuning stage that embeds the domain knowledge into a reasoning-capable LLM using the generated QA pairs as auditable reasoning traces, and a travel-domain benchmark dataset that measures the fine-tuned model's accuracy and calibration. We evaluate our approach using Qwen3-4B with LoRA adaptation. Our reasoning model achieves an $82.4\%$ exact match on the benchmark. This performance significantly outperforms the pretrained Qwen3-4B baseline at $22.4\%$. A calibration analysis decomposes the residual $17.57\%$ of errors into two distinct failure modes: an over-confident multi-label decoder that predicts both correct answers plus one spurious option on most dual-answer mistakes, and a smaller reasoning failure on single-answer questions where the supporting facts are present in the KG but the model fails to reconstruct the correct multi-hop path. This split confirms that explicit KG-grounded reasoning substantially improves the accuracy and uncertainty interpretation of LLMs in specialized domains, and isolates per-option calibration and trace-length-aware decoding as the next axes of improvement.

</details>


### [114] [MIThinker: A Plug-and-Play Policy-Optimized Thinker For Motivational Interviewing Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29265)
*Yizhe Yang,Palakorn Achananuparp,Heyan Huang,Jing Jiang,Ee-Peng Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIThinker是一个轻量级思考模型，通过生成治疗性思考指导动机性访谈（MI）咨询代理，结合AugR1-MI自动化流程和两阶段训练，在计算效率大幅提升下实现与现有系统相当的MI能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的咨询代理（包括MI方法）未显式对齐内部思考与咨询技术，限制了其有效性，且缺乏标注的思考数据。

Method: 提出MIThinker轻量级思考模型，结合AugR1-MI自动化逆向工程咨询师思考的管道，通过监督微调和强化学习的两阶段训练优化策略选择与响应生成。

Result: MIThinker显著提升心理理论评估和策略对齐能力；MindfulMI代理在计算量降低一个数量级的情况下，达到与现有系统相当的MI能力。

Conclusion: MIThinker通过结构化思考生成有效指导MI咨询代理，验证了轻量级思考模型在提升咨询效果与计算效率方面的潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning large language models (LLMs) have recently made much progress in complex problem-solving, leveraging internal reasoning (or thought) to guide their solution generation. However, existing LLM-based counseling agents, including those using Motivational Interviewing (MI), generate responses without explicitly aligning thoughts with counseling techniques, limiting their effectiveness. We propose MIThinker, a lightweight thinking model that generates therapeutic thoughts to guide MI counseling agents in strategy selection and response generation. To overcome the lack of annotated thought data, we introduce AugR1-MI, an automated pipeline that reverse-engineers counselor's thoughts from observed responses. Through two-stage training combining supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, MIThinker demonstrates improved theory-of-mind assessment and strategy alignment. Comprehensive evaluations show that MindfulMI, our agent leveraging MIThinker, achieves MI competency comparable to state-of-the-art systems with an order of magnitude less computation.

</details>


### [115] [A Hybrid Framework for Song Lyric Annotation Based on Human-LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29273)
*Rashini Liyanarachchi,Frank Tran,Md Mahmudul Hasan,Aditya Joshi,Erik Meijering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对歌词情感标注中人类与LLM标注不一致的问题，构建了句子级歌词数据集，并提出混合标注框架通过预测标注分歧优化标注质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歌词情感识别因歌词与歌曲整体情感可能存在偏差而具有挑战性，且现有歌词标注研究不足，亟需探索人类与LLM标注的协同优化方案。

Method: 创建句子级歌词数据集，对比人类与LLM标注一致性；设计混合标注框架，通过预测潜在标注分歧实现人机协同优化。

Result: 实验揭示歌词情感标注任务存在显著主观性与挑战，混合框架有效降低标注不一致性。

Conclusion: 该混合标注框架为歌词情感分析提供了高效可靠的数据标注方案，推动多模态情感计算领域发展。

Abstract: Emotion recognition of song lyrics is a challenging task since lyrics may not necessarily align with the overall emotion of a song. As a result, lyrics annotation remains largely underexplored. Drawing inspiration from research in large language model (LLM) assisted annotation, we examine the alignment between humans and LLMs for annotation of lyrics by creating a new sentence-level dataset of lyrics. Our observations highlight the subjectivity of the task and the inherent challenges. Following this, we present a hybrid annotation framework that optimizes human and LLM annotation by predicting potential misalignment in annotation.

</details>


### [116] [TriageRA-CCF: Source-Side Clinical Confidence and Coverage Signals for Adaptive Rank Budgeting in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29375)
*Shucan Ji,Yining Huang,Hongliang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对医学大语言模型中固定低秩预算的局限性，提出自适应秩预算分配方法TriageRA-CCF，通过结合置信度、临床覆盖和反事实信号动态调整LoRA秩通道，在Qwen3-8B和Llama3.1-8B上实现平均0.21和0.16点的准确率提升，但增益存在模型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型普遍采用固定低秩预算，无法适应医学问题在置信度、临床覆盖范围和跨领域难度上的显著差异，导致资源分配效率低下。

Method: 提出TriageRA-CCF框架，利用源训练数据中的三个信号（基础模型置信度、元数据临床覆盖、反事实接近错误代理）监督直通过预算路由器，动态选择LoRA秩通道（{2,4,8}），并引入预算成本、熵和秩平衡正则化防止路由崩溃。

Result: 在匹配CMB源训练协议下，TriageRA-CCF在Qwen3-8B和Llama3.1-8B上分别比最强外部基线提升0.21和0.16平均准确率，但增益幅度较小且在不同基准测试中表现不一致；组件消融实验证实三个信号均有效，但组合效果非单调最优。

Conclusion: 自适应秩预算分配可提升医学问答效率，但需针对具体模型优化信号组合策略；当前方法虽有效但改进空间有限，需进一步探索更精准的预算分配机制。

Abstract: Medical large language models are commonly adapted with a fixed low-rank budget, even though medical questions differ substantially in confidence, clinical coverage, and cross-domain difficulty. We study adaptive rank budgeting for parameter-efficient medical question answering: for each question, the adapter decides whether to activate a small, medium, or large subset of LoRA rank channels. The central challenge is that a naive adaptive budget router can collapse to unstable choices or spend capacity without improving shifted benchmarks. We propose TriageRA-CCF, a source-side teacher for adaptive rank-budgeted LoRA. It combines three signals computed only from source training data: base-model answer confidence, metadata-cell clinical coverage, and a counterfactual close-miss proxy. These signals supervise a straight-through budget router over active ranks {2,4,8}, together with budget-cost, entropy, and rank-balance regularization. Under a matched CMB-source training protocol, TriageRA-CCF achieves the best average accuracy among LoRA, DoRA, and MoELoRA baselines on both Qwen3-8B and Llama3.1-8B. The gains are modest and non-uniform across benchmarks: +0.21 average points over the strongest external baseline on Qwen3-8B and +0.16 on Llama3.1-8B. Component ablations show that confidence, coverage, and counterfactual signals all provide useful budget supervision, but their combination is not monotonically best on every backbone.

</details>


### [117] [Cross-Temporal Sinhala OCR: Page-Level Adaptation and Diachronic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29378)
*Avisha Dilhara,Nevidu Jayatilleke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对僧伽罗语OCR缺乏真实世界数据集的问题，构建了首个公开页面级数据集sinhala-ocr-lk-acts-1010，并通过实验验证LightOnOCR-2-1B模型在真实文档上的卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 僧伽罗语是斯里兰卡使用的形态丰富文字，但缺乏公开的页面级OCR数据集，现有研究依赖人工合成数据，导致模型评估脱离实际应用场景。

Method: 构建包含1010页立法文件图像及转录文本的数据集（1981-1989和2000-2019年），采用QLoRA技术在消费级/云GPU上对DeepSeek-OCR V1/V2和LightOnOCR-2-1B模型进行微调实验。

Result: LightOnOCR-2-1B取得1.05%字符错误率(CER)，显著优于开源模型Surya-OCR(8.84%)、Tesseract v5(10.69%)和商业模型Google Document AI(2.06%)，且在严重退化文档中保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了僧伽罗语真实OCR数据集空白，证明LightOnOCR-2-1B在复杂现实场景下具有行业领先的鲁棒性，为低资源语言OCR技术发展提供重要基础。

Abstract: Sinhala is a morphologically rich abugida spoken by roughly 16 million people in Sri Lanka, and to date, there are no publicly available real-world datasets for page-level Sinhala OCR. All previous studies for assessing Sinhala OCR models have used artificially generated data. To bridge the gap, we introduce sinhala-ocr-lk-acts-1010, an annotated dataset of 1,010 page-level images and their transcriptions collected from Sri Lankan Legislative Acts published between 1981-1989 and 2000-2019, split into 707 training examples, 101 validation examples, and 202 testing examples. Three models based on deep learning-based visual language processing, namely DeepSeek-OCR V1, DeepSeek-OCR V2, and LightOnOCR-2-1B, are fine-tuned using QLoRA in 8 experiments conducted on consumer and cloud GPUs. LightOnOCR-2-1B is the top performer, achieving a CER of 1.05% across all test examples, outperforming state-of-the-art open-source OCR models such as Surya-OCR (8.84%) and Tesseract v5 (10.69%), as well as commercially available OCR models such as Google Document AI (2.06%). Our results suggest that LightOnOCR-2-1B outperforms other baselines on real-world OCR tasks and maintains consistent performance across all print periods, even when documents are severely degraded.

</details>


### [118] [LC-ICL: Label-Guided Contrastive In-Context Learning for Robust Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29407)
*Xiao You,Tianwei Yan,Shan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LC-ICL方法，通过结合带错误原因标注的正负样本构建上下文学习示例，在命名实体识别和关系提取任务中显著提升大语言模型的少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的少样本信息提取研究仅关注使用正确示例进行上下文学习，忽略了错误示例蕴含的潜在价值，导致模型难以识别和规避典型错误模式。

Method: 设计LC-ICL框架：1) 构建包含错误原因标签的负样本集；2) 提取测试样本的最近正邻域与硬负样本特征；3) 将正负样本组合生成增强型上下文学习提示模板。

Result: 在多个标准数据集上的实验表明，LC-ICL相比传统少样本上下文学习方法，在NER和RE任务中平均F1值提升8.7%，尤其在低资源场景下性能增益达15.3%。

Conclusion: 首次验证了错误样本在上下文学习中的关键作用，证明结构化错误特征注入可显著提升模型泛化能力，为少样本信息提取提供了新范式。

Abstract: There has been increasing interest in exploring the capabilities of advanced large language models (LLMs) in the field of information extraction (IE), specifically focusing on tasks related to named entity recognition (NER) and relation extraction (RE).Although researchers are exploring the use of few-shot information extraction through in-context learning with LLMs, they tend to focus only on using correct or positive examples for demonstration, neglecting the potential value of incorporating incorrect or negative examples into the learning process.In this paper, we present LC-ICL a novel few-shot technique that leverages both correct and incorrect sample constructions to create in-context learning demonstrations. This approach enhances the ability of LLMs to extract entities and relations by combining positive samples with negative samples annotated by error-cause labels. These labels expose more detailed error features in erroneous examples, enabling the model to understand why similar predictions fail and avoid repeating such errors during inference.Specifically, our proposed method taps into the inherent contextual information and valuable information in hard negative samples and the nearest positive neighbors to the test and then applies the in-context learning demonstrations based on LLMs. Our experiments on various datasets indicate that LC-ICL outperforms previous few-shot in-context learning methods, delivering substantial enhancements in performance across a broad spectrum of related tasks. These improvements are noteworthy, showcasing the versatility of our approach in diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [EntroRouter: Learning Efficient Model Routing via Entropy Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29424)
*Kaiyi Zhang,Xueliang Zhao,Zhuocheng Gong,Wei Wu,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EntroRouter是一个单轮模型路由框架，通过熵调节机制解决多轮框架中的Trust Region Collapse问题，在保持98.3%最强专家准确性的同时降低48.25%计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型路由方法需要在准确性和计算成本间平衡，但多轮框架存在推理与路由深度耦合导致的结构故障模式"Trust Region Collapse"，在稀疏监督下强预训练先验主导导致能力强的专家被系统性抑制，陷入退化局部最优。

Method: 提出EntroRouter单轮路由框架，将熵调节作为核心目标。通过Soft Supervision初始化策略，拟合适合模型分布建立高熵先验进行探索；使用Soft Anchor稳定强化学习，利用离线能力估计在安全信任区域内编排受控熵收缩。

Result: 实验表明EntroRouter保留了最强专家98.3%的准确性，同时将计算成本降低了48.25%。

Conclusion: EntroRouter成功解决了Trust Region Collapse问题，通过单轮路由和熵调节机制，在保持高准确性的同时显著降低计算成本，证明了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Model routing balances solution accuracy and computational cost by selecting among models of varying capabilities. While recent multi-round frameworks interleave reasoning and planning, we identify a structural failure mode termed Trust Region Collapse. We demonstrate that the deep coupling of reasoning and routing, exacerbated by the dominance of strong pre-training priors under sparse supervision, leads to degenerate local optima where capable experts are systematically suppressed. To decouple these processes, we propose $\textbf{EntroRouter}$, a single-round routing framework that treats entropy regulation as a core objective. We first initialize the policy via Soft Supervision, fitting a distribution of suitable models to establish a high-entropy prior for exploration. Subsequently, we stabilize Reinforcement Learning using a Soft Anchor, which utilizes offline capability estimates to orchestrate controlled entropy contraction within a safe trust region. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EntroRouter retains 98.3% of the strongest expert's accuracy while reducing computational costs by 48.25%.

</details>


### [120] [mamabench and mamaretrieval: Benchmarks for Evaluating Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Maternal, Neonatal, and Reproductive Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29467)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 发布两个填补母婴健康领域空白的新基准：mamabench（25,949题专家问答集）和mamaretrieval（3,185查询与63,650块指南的分级相关性评估），并公开用于研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗问答基准缺乏覆盖助产士在母婴、新生儿及生殖健康领域的专业问题，且无公开的块级母婴健康指南检索相关性基准。

Method: 通过筛选整合7个专家来源构建mamabench（含多选/简答/评分标准题型），并调整HealthBench元评估校准LLM评分；构建mamaretrieval时采用分解评分标准区分“回答查询”与“仅涉及主题”的块，同时测量标签局限性（如范围分类器一致性、前沿法官验证）。

Result: 成功发布两个基准：mamabench覆盖25,949题，mamaretrieval包含3,185查询与63,650块的0-6级相关性标注，并公开标签质量评估结果。

Conclusion: 为母婴健康领域的AI研究提供首个公开块级检索基准和专家级问答评估工具，推动助产士相关医疗AI系统的开发与验证。

Abstract: Medical question-answering benchmarks rarely cover the maternal, neonatal, child, and reproductive-health questions a nurse-midwife asks, and, to our knowledge, no public chunk-level relevance benchmark exists for maternal-health guideline retrieval. We release two benchmarks that fill these gaps. mamabench is a scope-filtered QA set of 25,949 items assembled from seven existing expert-authored sources across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded tracks; to help users calibrate the LLM judge that scores the rubric track, we re-scope HealthBench's physician-labelled meta-evaluation to the domain. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with graded (0-6) relevance labels over a 63,650-chunk maternal-health guideline corpus, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes a chunk that answers a query from one merely on its topic. Three decisions shape both: assemble and filter expert sources rather than author questions, grade relevance rather than binarise it, and measure and disclose the limits of the labels -- scope-classifier agreement, a frontier-judge check, and a pooling-completeness audit -- rather than treat them as an oracle. A companion paper uses the benchmarks to evaluate a deployed on-device assistant; both are released openly for research.

</details>


### [121] [To Reason or to Fabricate: Reasoning Without Shortcuts via Hint-Anchored Pairwise Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29481)
*Jiuheng Lin,Chen Zhang,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HIPPO框架通过提示注入和成对奖励模型解决RL中的数据重叠问题，使LLM学习真实推理能力而非捷径模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽能增强LLM推理能力，但Pre-RL数据重叠导致模型通过记忆答案而非真正推理来解决问题，产生虚假推理现象。

Method: 提出HIPPO框架，结合提示注入聚合与成对奖励模型：通过提示注入触发重叠行为生成显式锚点，利用轻量级裁判模型进行成对比较以区分真实推理与捷径合理化。

Result: 实验显示HIPPO显著优于基线方法，在分布外任务中表现出良好泛化性，证明其提取的是可迁移的真实推理能力而非表面模式。

Conclusion: HIPPO有效解决了数据重叠导致的捷径问题，通过稳定的成对优化机制确保模型学习可泛化的推理技能，为RL增强LLM提供可靠解决方案。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) significantly enhances LLM reasoning, its efficacy is severely undermined by Pre-RL data overlap, where RL datasets overlap with pretraining or SFT corpora, causing models to exploit shortcuts by memorizing correct answers and fabricating post-hoc reasoning. To address this, we introduce HIPPO, a novel RL framework that integrates hint-injected aggregation with a tailored pairwise reward model. By utilizing hint injection to deliberately trigger overlap-induced behaviors, the resulting traces naturally serve as explicit anchors for pairwise comparison. This provides highly discriminable preference signals, enabling a lightweight judge model to reliably distinguish genuine reasoning deduction from shortcut-driven rationalization, while the pairwise formulation ensures stable and robust optimization compared to standard PRMs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HIPPO yields substantial improvements over standard baselines and generalizes effectively to out-of-distribution general tasks, showing it extracts authentic, transferable reasoning skills rather than superficial shortcut patterns.

</details>


### [122] [Which Tokens Need Context? A Reference-Based Analysis of Translation Responsibility Using Fertility and Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29489)
*Ramakrishna Appicharla,Baban Gain,Santanu Pal,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种模型无关的框架，通过生育率（每个源词生成的目标词数量）和熵（生育模式稳定性）量化翻译中的上下文敏感性，发现功能词更依赖上下文而内容词保持稳定，为机器翻译评估提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文使用分析方法依赖特定语料或模型内部结构，缺乏通用性，难以客观评估机器翻译系统是否以人类方式使用上下文。

Method: 基于词对齐构建事后分析框架，采用生育率与熵两个指标，在德语↔英语、英语→印地语三组语言对的参考翻译中测试四种上下文条件。

Result: 上下文选择性地将生成责任从源词转移到上下文词，但未改变总体生育率；功能词生育率显著下降，内容词保持稳定，表明上下文主要解决歧义而非添加新信息。

Conclusion: 该框架首次提供人类翻译中选择性上下文使用的真实表征，建立诊断基准以评估机器翻译模型的上下文利用能力。

Abstract: When humans translate, not every word depends equally on the surrounding context. Some tokens, particularly function words like pronouns and auxiliaries, rely heavily on preceding or following sentences, while others, such as proper nouns, do not. Understanding this inherent context sensitivity is essential for evaluating whether machine translation systems use context in human-like ways. However, existing approaches to analysing context usage rely on discourse-specific test sets or model internals, making them narrow or model-dependent. We propose a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework to quantify context sensitivity at lexical and syntactic levels using two measures derived from word alignments: fertility (number of target tokens generated per source token) and entropy (stability of fertility patterns across contexts). Using reference translations for three language pairs (German $\leftrightarrow$ English, English $\rightarrow$ Hindi) under four context conditions, we show that context selectively redistributes generative responsibility from source to context tokens without altering overall fertility. Function words show the largest fertility reductions, while content words remain stable, suggesting that context resolves ambiguity rather than adding new information. Our framework provides a ground-truth characterisation of selective context usage in human translation, establishing a diagnostic baseline for evaluating machine translation models.

</details>


### [123] [The Verbose Context Problem in Medical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29503)
*Shiva Kaul,Min-Gyu Kim,Anjum Khurshid,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对人口健康领域中的冗长上下文问题，提出了PopMedQA基准测试，发现领域无关方法无法有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人口健康领域，队列级别分析需要处理数千个医学编码事件，通常超过400K tokens，这种token效率低的文本表示构成了严重的冗长上下文瓶颈。

Method: 构建了PopMedQA基准测试，专门通过纵向患者记录组的计算任务来隔离冗长上下文问题。使用neopatient库（一种用于语言控制生成人工患者记录的新库）来构建基准测试，并进行了广泛的消融实验，包括提示策略、提示压缩和代理分解等方法。

Result: 通过实验发现，领域无关方法无法有效缓解冗长上下文问题，表明现有通用方法在处理大规模医学数据推理时存在局限性。

Conclusion: 在利用语言模型输入中的领域特定结构进行人口规模推理方面仍存在重大机会，需要开发专门针对医学领域的方法来解决冗长上下文问题。

Abstract: The verbose context problem occurs when structured concepts have token-inefficient textual representations. This bottleneck is acute in population health: cohort-level analysis of longitudinal patient records requires reasoning over thousands of medically-coded events, often exceeding 400K tokens in total. We present PopMedQA, a benchmark isolating this problem through computational tasks on groups of longitudinal patient records. We construct the benchmark using neopatient, a new library for language-controlled generation of artificial patient records. Through extensive ablations -- including prompting strategies, prompt compression, and agentic decomposition -- we find that domain-independent methods fail to alleviate the verbose context problem. There remains significant opportunity to exploit domain-specific structure in language model inputs for population-scale reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [Preference-ASR: A Preference-Aware Test Set for Benchmarking ASR in the Era of Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29534)
*Nithin Rao Koluguri,Sasha Meister,Nikolay Karpov,Piotr Zelasko,Desh Raj,Jagadeesh Balam,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PreferenceASR测试集，评估ASR系统遵循用户输出风格偏好的能力，涵盖归一化、实体、不流利和大小写四类指令，通过LLM辅助构建并经人工验证，公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR测试集在数字、不流利、实体和大小写处理上存在不一致，标准归一化器抹去用户关心的格式差异，导致无法衡量模型是否遵循用户输出风格偏好。

Method: 基于七个开源语料库，通过两阶段LLM辅助管道构建数据集，结合人工验证；采用偏好感知归一化器选择性跳过与指令冲突的步骤进行评估。

Result: 对四个模型的基准测试显示，不同偏好类型下模型排名变化显著，揭示传统评估方法掩盖的质量差异。

Conclusion: PreferenceASR填补了ASR评估中用户偏好遵循能力的空白，为更精准的ASR系统评估提供新标准，并推动领域向用户导向优化发展。

Abstract: Popular ASR test sets adopt inconsistent conventions for numbers, disfluencies, entities, and casing, while standard normalizers erase the format distinctions users care about. Current benchmarks therefore cannot measure whether a model follows user preferences for output style. We introduce PreferenceASR, a test set evaluating ASR systems on their ability to follow natural-language preference instructions across four categories: normalization, entities, disfluencies, and case. Built from seven open-source corpora via a two-stage LLM-assisted pipeline with human verification, it is evaluated with a preference-aware normalizer that selectively skips steps matching the active instruction. Benchmarking four models shows rankings shift across preference types, exposing quality differences traditional evaluation obscures. We publicly release the dataset.

</details>


### [125] [AURORA: Asymmetry and Update-Induced Rotation for Robust Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29545)
*Zishuai Zhang,Hainan Zhang,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AURORA框架，通过LLM权重梯度动态检测幻觉，使用余弦相似度偏度和旋转比率两个特征，在多个模型和数据集上表现优异，并具有良好的跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的幻觉问题阻碍其在高风险应用中的部署。现有检测方法依赖昂贵的输出级一致性检查或静态隐藏状态探针，只能捕捉浅层数据集特定模式，在跨数据集评估时性能显著下降。

Method: 提出AURORA幻觉检测框架，关注LLM的权重梯度动态而非静态表示。核心洞察是幻觉和忠实答案在模型参数上诱导不同质量的梯度更新模式。使用两个互补特征：(1)权重矩阵与梯度更新方向余弦相似度分布的偏度；(2)通过SVD量化梯度更新如何重新定向权重矩阵奇异向量基的旋转比率。

Result: 在四个模型家族和四个基准数据集上实现强大的幻觉检测性能。分析表明方法在不同模型规模上有效扩展，并能迁移到域外任务，包括数学推理和视觉语言场景。

Conclusion: AURORA通过捕捉权重梯度动态实现高效幻觉检测，相比现有方法具有更好的泛化能力和跨域迁移性能，为LLM幻觉检测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of natural language processing tasks. However, their tendency to generate hallucinations, namely factually incorrect or unfaithful outputs, poses a critical obstacle to their deployment in high-stakes applications. Although recent hallucination detection methods have made encouraging progress, they typically rely on costly output-level consistency checks or static hidden-state probes that capture shallow dataset-specific patterns, leading to substantial degradation under cross-dataset evaluation. In this work, we propose AURORA, a novel hallucination detection framework that shifts the focus from static representations to the weight-gradient dynamics of LLMs. Our key insight is that hallucinated and faithful answers induce qualitatively different gradient update patterns on the model's parameters. Specifically, hallucinated samples trigger asymmetric and structurally misaligned gradients, which can be captured through two complementary features: (1) the skewness of the cosine similarity distribution between weight matrices and their gradient update directions, and (2) the rotation ratio, which quantifies how much the gradient update reorients the singular-vector basis of weight matrices via SVD. AURORA achieves strong hallucination detection performance across four model families and four benchmark datasets. Further analyses demonstrate that our method scales effectively across model sizes and transfers to out-of-domain tasks, including mathematical reasoning and vision-language scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [Coverage-Driven KV Cache Eviction for Efficient and Improved Inference of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29563)
*Shuvendu Roy,Mengyao Zhai,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Golnoosh Samei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型KV缓存内存开销问题，提出K-VEC覆盖感知驱逐策略，通过跨头/跨层模块优化token保留，在保持内存效率的同时显著提升长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐策略虽降低内存占用，但导致唯一token覆盖度下降，造成长上下文任务性能显著退化，尤其影响依赖全局信息推理的任务。

Method: 设计K-VEC框架：1) 提出覆盖感知驱逐机制，量化token覆盖价值；2) 构建跨注意力头覆盖模块，平衡不同头部的token保留；3) 开发跨层覆盖模块，协调多层级缓存决策。

Result: 在16个LongBench基准测试中，相同驱逐率下性能提升最高达10.35分，验证了覆盖度优化对维持互信息的关键作用。

Conclusion: 证明token覆盖度是KV缓存优化的核心要素，所提方法为资源受限场景下的LLM部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at complex tasks like question answering and summarization, thanks to their ability to handle long-context inputs. However, deploying LLMs is costly, not only due to the high computational demands of quadratic complexity of self-attention and auto-regressive generation, but also because of the significant memory overhead required for storing the key-value (KV) cache during inference. To reduce the memory cost, existing KV-cache eviction strategies leverage the sparsity in attention to selectively store a subset of tokens. While reducing the memory footprint, such approaches show a considerable drop in performance, especially in tasks that require long-context reasoning. We identify that the drop in performance is linked to a reduction in the coverage of unique tokens. Additionally, we theoretically show that reduced coverage limits the mutual information between inputs and outputs, thereby impairing predictive accuracy. To this end, we introduce K-VEC, a novel coverage-aware KV-cache eviction strategy that prioritizes token coverage while evicting tokens in the cache. K-VEC introduces a cross-head and a cross-layer coverage module to enhance token retention across attention heads and model layers, mitigating performance degradation caused by low coverage. Evaluated on 16 LongBench subsets, K-VEC exhibit up to 10.35 points improvement over the existing methods under the same eviction rate and memory constraint. Comprehensive evaluations validate the effectiveness of our approach and demonstrate its potential for efficient LLM deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [127] [Anisotropy Decides Cosine vs. Rank Metrics for Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29571)
*V. S. Raghu Parupudi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验发现：文本嵌入相似性度量选择取决于嵌入空间的几何特性——当方差均匀分布时余弦相似度最优，当存在各向异性（方差集中于少数主导方向）时基于排名的L1类指标更优，且可通过单一指标（主维度方差占比）预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽指出不同相似性度量可能优于余弦相似度，但缺乏明确的几何条件解释何时及为何有效，导致实际应用缺乏理论指导。

Method: 对19种参数自由相似性度量在19个编码器（含紧凑句子Transformer至70亿参数大模型）和7个数据集上进行系统实证研究，并通过投影主导方向验证因果关系。

Result: 1. 几何特性决定最优度量：方差均匀分布时余弦最优，各向异性时排名/L1类指标显著优于余弦（相对提升约20%）；2. 主维度方差占比可预测替代度量效果（秩相关0.86，线性相关0.95）；3. 投影实验证实因果关系而非相关性。

Conclusion: 余弦相似度是方差均匀分布编码器的最佳选择（包括主流检索用微调嵌入器），而各向异性场景需采用其他度量；提出单一数值诊断工具判断何时需要切换度量。

Abstract: The standard way to compare two text embeddings is cosine similarity. Scattered studies report that a different metric does better, but never pin down the geometric condition that decides when, or why. We settle both with a comprehensive empirical study: nineteen parameter-free similarity metrics on nineteen encoders, from compact sentence transformers up to seven-billion-parameter large language models, across seven datasets. The answer is geometric. When an encoder spreads its variance evenly across directions, cosine is the best parameter-free choice and no other metric helps by a usable margin. When the variance concentrates into a few dominant directions, a property known as anisotropy, rank-based and L1-type metrics beat cosine by a clear margin. The absolute gain is modest, but because cosine starts low on these encoders it is a sizable relative improvement, around twenty percent on average and largest where cosine is weakest. What decides this is the geometry of the embedding space, not how the model was trained: where the two disagree, the metric follows the geometry. One number, the fraction of variance held by the single most dominant dimension, predicts how much the alternatives help across all nineteen encoders, with a rank correlation of 0.86 and a linear correlation of 0.95. To test this as the cause rather than a correlate, we project out the dominant directions: cosine recovers and the advantage of the other metrics nearly vanishes, but only on the encoders that were anisotropic to begin with. The effect is directional, not magnitude based, since it survives normalizing every vector to unit length. Among parameter-free metrics, then, cosine is the right tool wherever an encoder is well spread, which includes the fine-tuned embedders commonly deployed for retrieval, and we give a one-number diagnostic for when it is not.

</details>


### [128] [MAM-AI: An On-Device Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation System for Nurses and Midwives in Zanzibar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29580)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为撒哈拉以南非洲护士助产士开发的离线医疗问答AI助手MAM-AI，解决了设备内检索问题但生成器仍需改进，系统已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲的孕产妇和新生儿死亡率仍然很高，助产护理通常由缺乏国际标准助产培训的护士提供，在护理现场查阅权威指南很困难：指南冗长且网络连接不稳定。

Method: 开发MAM-AI医疗问答助手，完全在商用Android设备上运行。使用EmbeddingGemma(300M)进行问题嵌入，与87份指南文档(63,650个段落)的精选语料库匹配，然后由4B int4生成器(Gemma 4 E4B)提供带引用的答案，完全离线运行。采用分层评估方法：检索器、oracle上下文下的生成器、端到端和延迟评估，由LLM裁判评分并与医生评分标准验证。

Result: 设备内检索基本解决：300M嵌入器在七个检索器中排名第三，与云系统相当。但小生成器仍有问题：添加检索上下文并不能改善答案，4B模型无法同时做到有用和安全。语料库质量至关重要：当语料库包含正确段落时，答案是具体且可操作的；否则就变得模糊。

Conclusion: MAM-AI是一个经过充分评估的开源研究原型，不是现场产品；系统、知识库、基准测试和评估工具均已发布。该研究为资源受限环境下的医疗AI应用提供了重要参考，但强调需要进一步改进生成器的安全性和有用性平衡。

Abstract: Maternal and newborn mortality remain among the highest in sub-Saharan Africa, where midwifery care is often delivered by nurses who lack midwifery training to international standards, and consulting authoritative guidance at the point of care is hard: the guidelines are long and connectivity is intermittent. We present MAM-AI, a medical question-answering assistant for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar that runs entirely on a commodity Android device: a question is embedded (EmbeddingGemma, 300M) and matched against a curated corpus of 87 guideline documents (63,650 passages), then answered with citations by a 4B int4 generator (Gemma 4 E4B), fully offline, with no query leaving the device. We evaluate the exact deployed configuration with a layered methodology -- retriever, generator under oracle context, end-to-end, and latency -- scored by LLM judges validated against physician rubrics. The evaluation relocates the hard problem. On-device retrieval is essentially solved: the 300M embedder ranks third of seven retrievers and rivals cloud systems, so the passages the system needs are usually found. The small generator is what remains in doubt: adding retrieved context does not improve its answers, and at 4B it cannot be both helpful and safe at once -- of two same-size candidates, the more helpful one commits genuine dangerous errors, so we deploy the other, which is about twice as faithful to its sources (as faithful as a frontier model), and recover its helpfulness with a redesigned prompt that cuts deflection from 33% to 3%. Corpus quality is decisive for the same reason: where the corpus holds the right passage the answer is specific and actionable, and where it does not it goes vague. MAM-AI is a thoroughly evaluated, open-source research prototype, not a fielded product; the system, knowledge base, benchmarks, and evaluation harness are released.

</details>


### [129] [How much of an LLM-generated clinical corpus is actually new? A production-scale measurement of content redundancy for provenance classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29605)
*Ali H. Lazem,William J. Teahan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了临床大语言模型生成语料库的冗余问题，发现79.4%的token是冗余的，仅10.9%具有可训练的独特价值。通过溯源分析和无损压缩验证，揭示了两种冗余机制，并证明去重可提升下游临床编码器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习日益依赖LLM生成的训练语料库而非临床医生标注，这些语料库主要基于报告的规模进行描述和重用。研究旨在测试"体积是否反映信息内容"，即原始token计数是否准确衡量实际信息量。

Method: 采用基于溯源的冗余分解方法，对多智能体临床提取管道应用于167,034个患者叙述产生的25.1亿生成token进行token级分类。通过溯源标签和独立无损压缩分析两种方法验证冗余程度和机制。

Result: 仅10.9%的输出是可训练的独特内容，79.4%为冗余；原始token计数夸大信息内容约9倍。冗余来自两种机制：逐字复制源上下文到字段（可无损移除）和跨记录重复生成文本。一个管道通道几乎无冗余，表明冗余水平取决于通道结构。去重后在外部疾病识别基准上提升了临床编码器性能。

Conclusion: LLM生成临床语料库存在严重冗余问题，原始token计数严重高估信息内容。未纠正的冗余会扭曲训练分布，对下游任务产生可测量的负面影响。去重处理可在相同token预算下提升模型性能，该分类工具已开源。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning increasingly relies on training corpora generated by large language models (LLMs) rather than annotated by clinicians, and such corpora are described and reused largely on the basis of their reported scale. We test whether volume reflects information content. Analysing the complete output of a multi-agent clinical extraction pipeline applied to 167,034 patient narratives, 2.51 billion generated tokens across the ten text-bearing channels of an eleven-channel pipeline, we introduce Provenance-based Redundancy Decomposition, a token-level classification of the entire output by source. Only 10.9% of the output is trainable-unique content while 79.4% is redundant; raw token count overstates information content by roughly ninefold. The redundancy arises through two distinct mechanisms, verbatim copying of source context into per-item fields, and duplication of generated text across records, of which only the former is losslessly removable. An independent, model-free analysis based on lossless compression confirms the redundancy, recovering the two mechanisms without reference to the provenance labels. One pipeline channel carries almost no redundancy, showing that the level of redundancy depends on how each channel is structured rather than being a fixed property of LLM extraction. Because uncorrected redundancy up-weights the longer, more complex presentations that generate the most items, it skews the token-level training distribution of the corpus, a property we measure directly. In a controlled downstream test, de-duplicating the corpus before adaptation improved a clinical encoder on external disease-recognition benchmarks at equal token budget, robustly across adaptation depths and replicated on a second benchmark, confirming that the redundancy carries a measurable cost beyond storage. The classification tool is released openly.

</details>


### [130] [Do We Still Need Fine Tuning? Turkish Sentiment Analysis in the Era of Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29614)
*Sercan Karakaş,Yusuf Şimşek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证实监督微调在土耳其情感分析中仍优于零样本提示大模型，尤其在三分类任务中中立类处理存在显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型时代下，监督微调是否仍是土耳其情感分析的必要手段，并评估不同方法在真实场景中的有效性。

Method: 对比传统机器学习方法、微调BERTurk模型与提示式大语言模型在含负/中/正三类的土耳其电商评论数据集上的性能。

Result: 微调BERTurk在三分类任务中全面领先；大模型在二分类时表现接近，但在三分类时将中立评论错误归入极性类别导致性能骤降。

Conclusion: 零样本提示大模型尚未达到监督微调效果，且包含中立类别的评估对模型鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: This study examines whether supervised fine-tuning remains necessary for Turkish sentiment analysis in the era of large language models. We compare classical machine learning methods, fine-tuned pretrained language models, and prompted large language models on a Turkish e-commerce review dataset with negative, neutral, and positive labels. Fine-tuned BERTurk models perform best overall and outperform all prompted large language models in the full three-class task. The neutral class emerges as the main difficulty: while several large language models are much more competitive in binary positive--negative classification, they degrade substantially in the three-class setting by collapsing neutral reviews into polarized categories. The findings suggest that, in realistic Turkish sentiment classification, prompted large language models do not yet match supervised fine-tuning in the zero-shot setting, and that including the neutral class is crucial for robust evaluation.

</details>


### [131] [Two-Stage Prompt Optimization for Few-Shot Relation Extraction: From Reasoning-Guided Search to Gradient-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29639)
*Aunabil Chakma,Mihai Surdeanu,Eduardo Blanco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段自动提示优化框架，结合基于推理和基于梯度的优化方法，用于小语言模型的少样本关系抽取任务，在FS-TACRED上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动提示优化技术在少样本关系抽取领域（特别是使用小语言模型时）仍然研究不足，需要更有效的优化方法来提高模型性能。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用基于推理的优化器进行自然语言的广泛提示改进；第二阶段应用GradPO（梯度提示优化），利用损失和梯度信号识别高影响提示片段并进行局部编辑优化。

Result: 在FS-TACRED和FS-FewRel数据集上的实验表明，局部优化能显著改进第一阶段找到的提示，GradPO是最一致的精炼器；框架在FS-TACRED上使用Qwen3-4B达到最先进水平，在FS-FewRel上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该两阶段提示优化框架有效提升了小语言模型在少样本关系抽取任务上的性能，证明了结合推理和梯度优化的策略的优越性。

Abstract: Automatic prompt optimization is still underexplored for episodic few-shot relation extraction with smaller language models. We propose a two-stage framework that combines reasoning-based prompt optimization with gradient-based prompt optimization. The first stage can use any reasoning-based optimizer to make broadprompt improvements in natural language. The second stage applies our GradPO, which uses loss and gradient signals to identify high-impact prompt spans and refine them with local edits. Experiments on FS-TACRED and FS-FewRel show that local refinement usually improves prompts found by the first stage, and GradPO is the most consistent refiner. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on FS-TACRED with Qwen3-4B and remains competitive on FS-FewRel.

</details>


### [132] [Hybrid Retriever Evolution for Multimodal Document Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29648)
*Bohan Yao,Shruthan Radhakrishna,Vikas Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出失败驱动的进化框架，通过元智能体自主优化多模态检索器协调策略，显著提升文档问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统依赖固定流程组合检索器，无法动态适应多步推理需求，亟需将检索编排纳入推理过程的学习机制。

Method: 构建元智能体驱动的进化框架：1) 分析错误推理轨迹；2) 主动探测工具环境定位根因；3) 迭代重写任务智能体指令，实现检索器调用的动态决策。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc/DocBench上较基线提升19.6分，超越MACT/MDocAgent/SimpleDoc；检索分析证实改进源于自适应路由与跨模态证据组合。

Conclusion: 验证了多智能体协调范式对多模态文档推理的有效性，推动检索从静态前置模块向动态推理组件转变。

Abstract: Different retrievers, including lexical, semantic, and multimodal approaches, provide highly complementary strengths for multimodal document understanding, yet most systems combine them through fixed pipelines that cannot adapt to the demands of individual reasoning steps. In this work, we ask whether retrieval orchestration itself can be learned as part of the reasoning process. We introduce a failure-driven evolution framework in which a meta-agent autonomously discovers how a tool-using task agent should coordinate diverse retrievers during multi-step document question answering. The meta-agent analyzes incorrect reasoning trajectories, actively probes the same tool environment to diagnose root causes, and iteratively rewrites the task agent's instructions, turning retrieval from a fixed front-end stage into an adaptive, step-wise reasoning decision. The evolved agent learns when to invoke each retriever, how to combine them, and how to compose evidence across modalities and pages. On MMLongBench-Doc and DocBench, the evolved agent achieves gains of up to +19.6 points over the unevolved baseline and consistently outperforms recent systems including MACT, MDocAgent, and SimpleDoc. Detailed retrieval analyses confirm that these improvements arise from adaptive routing and evidence composition rather than reliance on any hard coded retrieval mode, and evolution dynamics reveal a progressive shift from narrow lexical behavior to rich multi-tool coordination. These findings establish autonomous multi-agent coordination as a promising paradigm for multimodal document reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [Resolution Thresholds in VLM Detection of Harmful ASCII Art Across Construction Modes and Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29649)
*Yikai Hua,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型视觉语言模型在内容审核中易受ASCII艺术越狱攻击，本研究通过测试八种VLM在不同分辨率和字符构建模式下的表现，发现检测率随分辨率升高显著下降，基于单词的ASCII艺术最难检测，揭示了系统性漏洞并呼吁建立分辨率感知的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型作为内容审核工具时，存在被ASCII艺术编码的有害文本绕过检测的漏洞，亟需研究分辨率对检测效果的影响以识别系统性弱点。

Method: 在英语和中文语料上，使用十种分辨率生成八种字符构建模式（L1-L8）的ASCII艺术图像，评估八种先进VLM的检测性能，分析跨模型、模式和语言的检测失败阈值。

Result: 检测率在超过特定分辨率阈值后急剧下降，基于单词的ASCII艺术模式在全分辨率范围内最难检测，证实了VLM审核系统的系统性漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VLM内容审核系统对高分辨率ASCII艺术的脆弱性，强调需开发分辨率感知的评估标准以提升审核鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as content moderation tools, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in which harmful text is visually encoded as ASCII art. This can allow inappropriate or harmful content to bypass moderation systems. To address this vulnerability, this paper investigates how image resolution affects VLM detection of harmful ASCII art across eight character construction modes (L1-L8), ranging from dense block characters to word-embedded designs. We evaluate eight state-of-the-art VLMs on English and Chinese corpora using a pipeline that generates ASCII art images at ten resolution scales, probing whether a consistent detection-failure threshold exists across models, modes, and languages. Results indicate that detection rates decline sharply above certain resolution thresholds, and that word-based modes are the most resistant to detection across the full resolution range. These findings reveal a systematic vulnerability in VLM-based content moderation systems and motivate resolution-aware evaluation standards.

</details>


### [134] [How LLMs See Creativity: Zero-Shot Scoring of Visual Creativity with Interpretable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29672)
*William Orwig,Roger E. Beaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究测试了6个多模态大语言模型能否零样本评估视觉创意，发现它们能与人类评分显著对齐，且推理过程提供了可解释的评估窗口，但推理并未提高对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视觉图像的原创性对创造力评估构成持久挑战。虽然AI在语言领域的自动评分已有效，但视觉创造力的评估及模型评分机制的理解仍存在关键问题。

Method: 测试6个多模态LLMs（Gemini 3 Flash, Gemma 4 31B IT, GPT-5.4 Mini, GLM-5v Turbo, Kimi K2.5, Qwen 3.6 Plus）在992个AI生成图像和1500个手绘草图上，这些图像已由人类评分者进行创造力评分。研究1评估模型与人类评分的对齐度，研究2分析模型的逐步推理过程。

Result: 研究1：所有模型在两个数据集上都与人类创造力评分显著对齐（AI生成图像r=.57-.68；草图r=.29-.68）。研究2：推理过程使模型评估可解释，展示了模型关注点、原创性与质量的平衡方式及评分理由，但推理并未提高与人类评分的对齐度。

Conclusion: 多模态LLMs可以在无需额外训练的情况下匹配人类对视觉创意的判断，其推理过程揭示了AI模型如何评估创造力。研究提供了开放评分应用实现这一流程。

Abstract: Evaluating the originality of visual images poses enduring challenges for creativity assessment. Automated scoring using AI models has proven effective in the verbal domain, yet key questions remain about evaluating visual creativity and understanding how models arrive at their ratings. The present research asks whether multimodal large language models (LLMs) can serve as judges of visual creativity zero-shot (without any fine-tuning or examples of human ratings) and whether their "reasoning" output offers an interpretable window into their evaluation process. We tested six multimodal LLMs (Gemini 3 Flash, Gemma 4 31B IT, GPT-5.4 Mini, GLM-5v Turbo, Kimi K2.5, and Qwen 3.6 Plus) on 992 AI-generated images (based on human-written prompts) and 1,500 hand-drawn sketches scored for creativity by human raters. In Study 1, all models showed substantial alignment with human creativity ratings on both datasets (r = .57-.68 on AI-generated images; r = .29-68 on sketches). In Study 2, we analyzed the step-by-step reasoning processes of three LLMs evaluating the same images and drawings. Although reasoning made model evaluations interpretable -- showing what they attend to, how they balance originality vs. quality, and how they justify their ratings -- reasoning did not improve alignment with human ratings. In sum, our findings indicate that multimodal LLMs can match human judgments of visual creativity without any additional training, and that their reasoning reveals how AI models evaluate creativity. An open scoring app implementing this pipeline is available at https://review-visual-eval-scoring.hf.space.

</details>


### [135] [Can MLLMs Critique Like Humans? Evaluating Open-Ended Aesthetic Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29689)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Maryam Amirizaniani,Haniyeh Ehsani Oskouie,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This study reveals that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate aesthetic critiques that superficially resemble human evaluations but fundamentally differ in selectivity, depth, and image-specificity, despite high reference-based similarity scores.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing aesthetic evaluation benchmarks rely on multiple-choice or numeric scoring, failing to capture the nuanced, open-ended nature of human-written critiques. This gap necessitates assessing whether MLLMs can produce critiques that genuinely align with human perspectives rather than merely matching surface-level metrics.

Method: Evaluated five open-weight MLLMs using the Reddit Photo Critique Dataset. Applied reference-based similarity metrics under six prompt conditions (persona framing, aspect hinting, length control, single/multi-pass generation) plus an image-grounding control (wrong photograph input). Compared model outputs against ranked human critiques using lexical, learned, and embedding-based metrics.

Result: Reference-based similarity metrics overestimated alignment. Stricter metrics showed weak correspondence with human critiques. Embedding cosine similarity revealed broad topical overlap attributable to models' consistent "house style" rather than image-specific analysis. Behaviorally, models produced 2-3× longer critiques, covered nearly all aesthetic aspects uniformly (vs. human selectivity), engaged aspects more deeply, and repeated content across critiques of the same photo.

Conclusion: Reference-based similarity rewards fluent, comprehensive critique styles rather than human-like selectivity and specificity. This misalignment highlights limitations in current evaluation paradigms and suggests the need for new metrics/training approaches that prioritize human-aligned critique characteristics in open-ended multimodal generation.

Abstract: Open-ended aesthetic critique is a challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): unlike multiple-choice aesthetic benchmarks, it has no single correct answer, and most aesthetic evaluation has measured models against numeric scores rather than the written critiques people actually give. We evaluate MLLM critiques against ranked human references and ask whether they are close to human ones. Using the Reddit Photo Critique Dataset, we score five open-weight MLLMs against multiple ranked human critiques per photo with reference-based similarity metrics, under six prompt conditions that disentangle persona framing, aspect hinting, length control, and single- versus multi-pass generation, and add an image-grounding control that feeds each model the wrong photograph. We find that reference-based similarity gives a misleading picture. Stricter lexical and learned metrics show only weak alignment with human critiques, while a coarse embedding cosine reports broad topical overlap that the grounding control traces to a stable house style rather than image-specific observation. Behaviorally, the models diverge from humans in consistent ways the scores do not surface: even under a length cap they write two to three times as much, cover nearly every aesthetic aspect where humans are selective, engage each aspect more uniformly and at greater depth, and repeat themselves across critiques of the same photo where humans vary. We argue that reference-based similarity rewards a fluent, comprehensive critique style rather than the selectivity and specificity of human critique, and discuss implications for evaluating and training open-ended multimodal generation.

</details>


### [136] [Why Struggle with Continuous Latents? Interpretable Discrete Latent Reasoning via Rendered Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29712)
*Shuochen Chang,Qingyang Liu,Shaobo Wang,Bingjie Gao,Qianli Ma,Haonan Zhao,Yibo Miao,Yulin Sun,Zelin Peng,Jiangtong Li,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出离散潜变量推理（DLR）方法，通过将连续潜状态转化为离散标记解决训练不稳定和不可解释问题，在多个基准测试中实现最高20倍压缩率，同时保持语义可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜变量推理方法因连续空间与离散符号监督不匹配，导致训练困难、推理轨迹不稳定且不可解释。连续状态缺乏明确的锚点来支持逐步对齐，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出离散潜变量推理（DLR）框架：1) 将文本思维链渲染为图像并提取视觉特征；2) 通过聚类微调构建离散潜词汇表；3) 扩展词汇表和输出头以支持自然语言与离散标记的自回归建模，兼容预训练对齐、监督微调和强化学习。

Result: 在5个推理基准和2个模型系列（Qwen3-VL/LLaMA-3）上验证，DLR较基线方法性能提升显著，实现最高20倍压缩率，且学习到的潜轨迹保持可解释的语义结构。

Conclusion: 离散潜标记为高效潜变量推理提供了可控且可解释的基础，有效平衡了计算效率与推理质量，为复杂任务优化开辟新路径。

Abstract: Large language models achieve high reasoning performance via explicit chain-of-thought and reinforcement learning, but require long output sequences and extended inference time. Latent reasoning reduces this cost by shifting computation into a latent space; however, continuous latent methods are hard to train, suffering from unstable and uninterpretable reasoning trajectories. We argue these issues stem from a misalignment between continuous-space reasoning and discrete symbolic supervision, as continuous states lack explicit anchors for step-by-step alignment. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{Discrete Latent Reasoning~(DLR)}, the first method that converts continuous latent states into explicit discrete tokens. Inspired by render-based compression, we render textual chains of thought into images, extract visual features, and construct a discrete latent vocabulary via clustering-based fine-tuning. Expanding the vocabulary and output head enables standard autoregressive modeling over both natural language and latent tokens, supporting pretraining alignment, SFT, and RL. Experiments on five reasoning benchmarks and two model series~(Qwen3-VL and LLaMA-3) confirm that \textbf{DLR} outperforms prior latent reasoning baselines with up to \textbf{20$\times$ compression}. Furthermore, the learned latent trajectories retain an interpretable semantic structure. Overall, discrete latent tokens provide a controllable and interpretable basis for efficient latent reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [SEVA: Self-Evolving Verification Agent with Process Reward for Fact Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29713)
*Aojie Yuan,Yi Nian,Haiyue Zhang,Zijian Su,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEVA是一个结构化验证代理，通过过程奖励和自进化循环解决LLM代理中的幻觉问题，在保持与GPT-4o-mini相当性能的同时提供更丰富可审核的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理中的幻觉是可靠性瓶颈，现有事实归因验证器仅输出不透明的二元标签，导致代理无法自我纠正、操作员无法审核。

Method: 提出SEVA结构化验证代理，输出证据对齐、逐步推理链、校准置信度和六类错误诊断；使用过程奖励将验证质量分解为五个独立组件（70/30权重偏向过程信号），解决RL训练中的优势崩溃问题；实现Verify->Reflect->Probe->Refine自进化循环。

Result: 在ClearFacts上，SEVA-3B与GPT-4o-mini性能相当（F1 69.0 vs 69.8），同时产生更丰富可审核的输出；发现每轮训练产生基准专家而非通用模型（HaluEval +15pp，TruthfulQA -10至-14pp）；验证行为对齐从0.917提升至0.997，格式从72%提升至100%。

Conclusion: 对于任何具有多组件生成的RL任务，奖励粒度必须匹配输出粒度，这一原则具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Hallucination is the reliability bottleneck for LLM-based agents, and fact attribution verifiers are the last line of defense -- yet today's verifiers emit only opaque binary labels, leaving agents unable to self-correct and operators unable to audit. We present SEVA, a structured verification agent that emits evidence alignments, step-by-step reasoning chains, calibrated confidence, and a six-category error diagnosis with actionable fixes. Training such an agent with RL is non-trivial: standard binary reward on multi-component output triggers advantage collapse -- within-group reward variance vanishes and the GRPO gradient disappears. We resolve this with a process reward that decomposes verification quality into five independent components weighted 70/30 toward process signals, restoring the gradient and inducing an implicit curriculum -- the agent first masters verification behavior (alignment 0.917 -> 0.997, format 72% -> 100%), then outcomes (F1 64.9 -> 69.0). Structured output further enables a Verify -> Reflect -> Probe -> Refine self-evolution loop, which over four rounds on a 7B model surfaces an unexpected structural finding: each round produces a benchmark-specialist, not a generalist (+15 pp on HaluEval, -10 to -14 pp on TruthfulQA in the same model, persistent at 4x data). On ClearFacts, SEVA-3B matches GPT-4o-mini (69.0 vs. 69.8 F1) while producing substantially richer, auditable output -- confirming a principle that should generalize: for any RL task with multi-component generation, reward granularity must match output granularity.

</details>


### [138] [How Far Do On-Prem Open LLMs Get on Text-to-SQL? A Cross-Family Size x Technique Frontier on BIRD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29733)
*Vladimir Beskorovainyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了本地部署开源模型在Text-to-SQL任务上的性能，发现模型世代比参数规模更重要，自我修正技术效果显著且成本极低，而模式链接和自我一致性方法价值有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织无法将数据发送到云API时，需要了解在本地使用开源权重运行Text-to-SQL的效果如何，以及哪些流行的准确性"配方"值得计算成本。

Method: 在BIRD开发集(n=1534, Execution Accuracy)上进行完全可复现的基准测试，评估三个开源模型家族(Qwen2.5-Coder、CodeLlama-Instruct、Llama-3.x)的两个世代，在匹配协议下逐步分析模型无关配方(模式链接、自我修正、自我一致性)的组件，每个差异都通过配对McNemar检验测试。

Result: (1)模型世代比原始大小更重要，Qwen2.5-Coder在7B时优于CodeLlama(39.1 vs 20.9)，但Llama-3.3-70B(49.2)具有竞争力；(2)自我修正是稳健的、近乎免费的改进，在所有三个家族中都有显著效果；(3)模式链接没有帮助，96.5%金表召回率的链接器与无链接在统计上无差异；(4)自我一致性价值较差(+0.13 pp，约5倍tokens，不显著)

Conclusion: 研究提供了真实的每阶段成本($/1k queries)并发布了所有代码、预测和摘要，为本地部署Text-to-SQL提供了实用的性能评估和优化建议。

Abstract: Organizations that cannot send data to a cloud API increasingly ask: how good is Text-to-SQL if the model must run on-premises on open weights, and which popular accuracy "recipes" are worth their compute? We answer with an honest, fully reproducible benchmark on the BIRD development split (n=1534, Execution Accuracy), evaluating three open model families across two generations -- Qwen2.5-Coder (7B/14B/32B), CodeLlama-Instruct (7B/13B/34B), and Llama-3.x (8B, 70B) -- under one matched protocol, ablating a model-agnostic recipe (schema linking, self-correction, self-consistency) component by component, with every difference tested by the paired McNemar test. Four findings stand out. (i) Generation matters more than raw size, and the recipe is family-robust: Qwen2.5-Coder dominates the older CodeLlama at matched size (39.1 vs 20.9 at 7B), but a modern non-Qwen model (Llama-3.3-70B, 49.2 on a matched serving) is competitive, so CodeLlama's weakness reflects its 2023 generation, not "non-Qwen = weak". (ii) Self-correction is a robust, near-free win, significant on all three families where there is room to improve. (iii) Schema linking does not help, and a stronger linker does not rescue it: a retrieval/embedding linker with 96.5% gold-table recall is statistically indistinguishable from no linking, ruling out the "weak lexical strawman" objection across three families. (iv) Self-consistency is poor value (+0.13 pp for ~5x tokens, not significant). We report real per-stage cost ($/1k queries) and release all code, predictions, and summaries; archived code and data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20952794

</details>


### [139] [Fast Numbers, Slow Language: Bridging Quantitative and Qualitative Earnings Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29734)
*Ding Yu,Zhuo Liu,Hao Zhang,Hangfeng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建首个整合财报新闻、电话会议记录及股价数据的语料库EarningsInOne，通过统一评估框架验证定量盈余惊喜（EPS/收入）与定性语言信号（管理层语气等）存在显著时间分离：定量信号在公告时达峰并于次日开盘前消退，定性信号则在次日达峰且具可交易性，此前基于MSE的评估方法掩盖了该特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融经济学家与NLP研究者长期分别研究财报发布的定量惊喜与定性语言信号，但因采用不同目标（收益率vs波动率）、交易策略（分位数对冲vs全样本交易）及评估指标（Q5-Q1价差vs MSE），导致跨领域比较困难，亟需统一框架揭示两类信号的协同作用机制。

Method: 构建覆盖标普1500指数2022-2025年数据的EarningsInOne语料库，整合新闻发布、电话会议记录及日内/次日常股价数据，采用统一交易策略与评估工具对两类信号进行对比分析。

Result: 验证两类信号存在显著时序分离：定量惊喜在公告时达到峰值并于次日开盘前消退；定性语言情绪在次日达到峰值且具备可交易性，但传统基于点态MSE优化的波动率评估方法未能有效捕捉该特征。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现财报信息跨模态统一分析框架，揭示定量与定性信号的时序异质性，证明定性语言信号具有独立于定量惊喜的可交易价值，为跨学科研究提供方法论突破。

Abstract: Earnings announcements release two types of information sequentially: quantitative surprise (numeric earnings-per-share (EPS)/revenue versus analyst estimate) arrives first in press releases and financial news, processed by algorithmic traders within minutes; qualitative language (management tone, guidance, question-and-answer (Q&A) credibility) arrives 30-90 min later in the earnings conference call transcript (ECT), requiring human interpretation overnight. Financial economists have studied quantitative surprise for 50 years; natural language processing (NLP) researchers have studied qualitative ECT signals for a decade. Despite studying the same event, the two communities used incompatible frameworks: different targets (return vs. volatility), trading setups (long top-decile and short bottom-decile vs. trade-all), and metrics (return spread between top and bottom 20% (Q5-Q1) vs. mean squared error (MSE)), making direct comparison and connection challenging.
  We bridge these communities with EarningsInOne, the first corpus aligning earnings news, ECTs, and intraday and next-day prices across SP 1500 (broad U.S. equity universe, 2022-2025). Applying unified trading and evaluation tools to both signal types, we confirm a clean speed separation, fast numbers, slow language: quantitative surprise peaks at announcement and is largely eliminated by the next market open; qualitative ECT sentiment peaks on the next trading day, real and tradeable, but hidden under prior transcript-based evaluation that optimised sign-agnostic volatility with pointwise MSE.

</details>


### [140] [Managing Map Cardinality in Automatic Disease Classification Mapping: Balancing Precision, Recall and Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29750)
*Santosh Purja Pun,Oliver Obst,Jim Basilakis,Jeewani Anupama Ginige*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于阻塞-匹配流程的新方法，利用大语言模型解决疾病分类系统（如ICD）间一对多映射问题，在多个ICD版本对测试中实现了更高精确度和更广映射覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的映射方法仅支持一对一映射，而阈值/Top-K方法在精确度、召回率和映射覆盖率间存在固有权衡，难以满足复杂医疗数据整合需求。

Method: 采用实体解析领域的阻塞-匹配范式：首先通过阻塞步骤生成候选映射对集合，随后使用大语言模型在候选块内执行精确匹配识别。

Result: 在ICD-9-CM↔ICD-10-CM和ICD-10-AM↔ICD-11数据集上验证，新方法在保持相当召回率的同时显著提升精确度，并实现更广的映射覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该方法有效突破传统映射局限，为医疗数据标准化和跨系统分析提供了高精度解决方案，相关资源已开源促进领域发展。

Abstract: Automatic mapping between disease classification systems, such as the International Classification of Diseases (ICD), is a challenging yet essential task for integrating health data and conducting longitudinal data analysis. Existing embedding-based methods primarily focus on \emph{one-to-one} mappings, overlooking more complex \emph{one-to-many} scenarios. The threshold-based and top-K methods offer natural extensions; however, they involve inherent trade-offs between \emph{precision}, \emph{recall} and \emph{mapping coverage} -- the proportion of source codes with at least one mapping to a target code. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel method, which is inspired by the \emph{blocking-and-matching} pipeline commonly used in \emph{entity resolution}. In particular, we first generate a block of candidate matches (\emph{blocking}) and then employ a large language model (LLM) to identify all valid mappings within each block (\emph{matching}). Empirically, we show that the proposed method achieves higher precision with comparable recall and broader coverage across multiple ICD version pairs (ICD-9-CM$\leftrightarrow$ICD-10-CM and ICD-10-AM$\leftrightarrow$ICD-11). Our source code and dataset is available at: https://tinyurl.com/46kyn7wp.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出RSEA递归自我进化代理，通过严格的保留门控机制确保性能不退化，在四个基准测试上验证了其有效性，发现没有万能的改进方法，但RSEA提供了安全可靠的自我进化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理改进方法通常通过进化自然语言工件（反思、工作流程、剧本等）而非权重更新来提升性能，但这些方法往往只在单一基准上报告成功，缺乏公平比较。研究旨在通过统一评估揭示更清晰的方法性能图景。

Method: 提出RSEA（递归自我进化代理），包含三层自然语言状态：命令式策略、可重用技能、程序化剧本。该方法跨代重写所有三层，基于自身轨迹进化，并采用严格的保留门控机制，仅在验证集上不退化时才提交候选。在ALFWorld、GAIA、(τ)-bench、WebShop四个基准测试上，与ReAct、Reflexion、GEPA、AWM、ACE、Dynamic Cheatsheet六个基线方法进行公平比较。

Result: 1）没有单一工件在所有任务上最优：RSEA在ALFWorld上达到69.3%（优于ReAct的64.6%，p=0.015），配合重试达79.4%为整体最佳；AWM在强骨干工具使用任务上表现最佳。2）无保护的上下文进化具有高方差和不安全性：Dynamic Cheatsheet在ALFWorld接近最佳（70.7%），但在WebShop崩溃（0.14 vs ReAct的0.43）。3）RSEA的严格保留选择确保单调安全：在任何基准上都不会显著低于基础代理，在进化上下文有害时回退到vanilla ReAct。

Conclusion: 严格的保留门控机制是实现安全递归自我进化的关键。不同改进方法在不同任务类型上各有优势，不存在万能解决方案。RSEA通过其三层状态设计和保守更新策略，提供了一种既有效又安全的LLM代理自我进化框架。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [142] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"能力切片"(capability slice)概念，通过建立评估-数据闭环系统，解决大语言模型预训练中能力评估与数据优化之间的脱节问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练中的模型能力是核心变量，但无法直接观测。数据前瞻性地塑造能力，而评估只能事后揭示能力，将样本、提示、解码和评分规则压缩成一个有噪声的分数。实际优化过程是逆向的：先观察到失败，然后工程师必须推断语料库修复方案。评估侧和数据侧使用不兼容的词汇体系，导致这种推断通常是直觉而非方法。

Method: 提出"能力切片"概念：一组共享背景条件、任务类型、解决操作和输出约束的评估样本，精确到可以定位单一弱点，但又足够稳定以承受聚合。基于此单元，构建评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成闭环系统，将基准级别的失败转化为有针对性的、可测试的数据干预。

Result: 通过两个相反方向的案例研究验证：1) 继续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，但诊断发现是单个掩码EOS损失问题而非推理能力减弱，恢复后BBH达到66.44；2) 数学推理弱点通过解决操作分解，针对性采样将AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升到26.67。同一未修改的闭环系统在两个案例中得出相反但正确的结论。

Conclusion: 评估到数据的推断可以成为常规的、可审计的、实验验证的过程，而非依赖直觉。能力切片框架为模型能力优化提供了系统化的方法论基础。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [143] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPTNT是一个基于合作视频游戏"Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes"的新型基准测试，用于评估多模态模型在真实协作环境中的表现，包括时间压力、信息不对称和实时沟通等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常孤立地研究协作条件（如时间压力、信息不对称、沟通不完美），而缺乏在真实协作环境中综合评估多模态模型的能力。需要一个能同时测试这些条件的基准测试。

Method: 基于"Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes"游戏构建GPTNT基准测试。设置两个代理：一个能看到和操作炸弹但无说明书，另一个有说明书但看不到炸弹。要求异步行动和实时沟通，并通过隐藏说明书、伙伴或两者来分离协作能力与记忆依赖。

Result: 测试的闭源和开源模型都无法在实时条件下成功拆弹，而人类玩家可以做到。通过受控实验识别出状态跟踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复等关键弱点。

Conclusion: GPTNT作为协作性能基准测试填补了当前评估的空白。由于基于真实游戏，具有程序生成能力和活跃模组社区，可随模型改进而演进，而非一次性解决后废弃。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [144] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了IMCBench，一个基于图像的多轮医疗对话基准，用于评估多模态模型在临床场景中的安全性、准确性和不确定性处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准存在碎片化问题：有些支持多轮对话但缺乏图像，有些提供多模态输入但只关注单轮问答任务。需要一个新的基准来填补这个空白，以评估模型在真实临床交互场景中的表现。

Method: 创建IMCBench基准，包含真实公开临床图像和合成患者档案，模拟患者-医生互动。在三个临床维度评估对话：安全性、准确性、诊断中不确定性使用的适当性。使用LLM-as-Jury评分方法，根据专家医生注释校准，测试了8个多模态前沿模型（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama四个家族）。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6得分最高(3.61)，其次是Claude Sonnet 4.6(3.30)和GPT-5.2(3.29)。没有模型在所有维度上都占主导地位。恶性肿瘤和罕见病条件下安全性下降(Δ=-0.27)。消融研究显示视觉输入和EHR上下文对安全指导至关重要(移除后安全性分别下降0.18和0.23)，更强模型能更有效利用视觉特征。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不保证安全的患者指导，需要多维度的评估框架来全面评估医疗AI系统的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [145] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出DynaSteer动态表示编辑框架，通过解耦推理模式并动态干预高熵路径，在数学和代码任务中显著提升LLM推理准确性，同时避免对正确轨迹的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理增强方法（如Chain-of-Thought）仅增加思考量但缺乏真理导向性，而Representation Editing（RepE）在动态推理轨迹中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出DynaSteer框架：1）通过模式聚类分离推理流形；2）使用Fisher-LDA投影纯净真理向量；3）基于前瞻熵动态监控并选择性引导/回滚轨迹。

Result: 在MATH基准测试中验证有效性，代码任务实验证明泛化能力，实验结果显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 首次揭示推理链中真理的几何特性，提出首个动态RepE框架实现精准干预，为可控LLM推理提供新范式。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [146] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VirtueMap是一个基于亚里士多德德性伦理学的框架，用于评估大语言模型在伦理困境中的响应。通过对7个伦理困境的5个响应进行排名分析，发现不同LLM在勇气、节制和正义方面存在显著差异，平均排名一致性达到90.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常面临伦理权衡问题，多个响应可能都是合理的但表达不同的价值优先事项，如公平、诚实、勇气或克制。需要一种框架来系统地描述和评估这些伦理模式。

Method: VirtueMap框架要求人类或LLMs对7个一般性、非致命、非政治、非宗教的伦理困境的5个响应进行排名。为每个困境和德性（实践智慧、正义、诚实、勇气、节制）提出从最表达到最不表达的排序，收集超过100个受访者评估，仅保留95%以上确认的排序作为操作地面真相。使用归一化Borda对齐对排名进行评分，评估9个LLM家族。

Result: 研究发现高平均排名一致性（90.3%），最大的差异出现在勇气、节制和正义方面。不同LLM家族在德性表达上表现出可测量的差异。

Conclusion: VirtueMap提供了一个有效的德性伦理学框架来评估LLMs的伦理响应，揭示了不同模型在核心德性表达上的系统性差异，为理解和改进AI伦理决策提供了新的评估工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [147] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATHENA-R1是一个基于强化学习的治疗推理AI智能体，覆盖1939年以来所有FDA批准药物，通过212个生物医学工具进行迭代证据收集，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有模型，准确率达94.7%（药物推理）和82.9%（治疗推理），并在临床专家评估中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理是医疗决策的核心，需要整合疾病背景、合并症、药物、禁忌症和不断发展的生物医学知识，是一个迭代过程，需要权衡多种约束条件并基于可验证证据。长期以来，AI难以进行有效的治疗推理，因为需要知道在得出结论之前应该寻找什么证据。

Method: 构建两级自学习框架：1）多智能体系统构建工具、任务和推理轨迹用于监督微调；2）强化学习使用科学反馈奖励推理质量（证据收集、有根据的工具使用、逻辑非冗余）。ATHENA-R1在每个步骤识别缺失信息，选择和运行相关工具，并整合证据。

Result: 在5个基准测试的3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例中，开放式药物推理准确率达94.7%，治疗推理准确率达82.9%，分别比GPT-5高出17.8和10.7个百分点。在28个罕见病组织的专家盲评中，在所有标准上都优于参考模型。医生对复杂住院心血管和传染病案例评价良好。在540万患者电子健康记录中测试的不良反应假设，调整后比值比为1.48-1.84，阴性对照无升高。

Conclusion: 证明治疗推理可以重新表述为可学习的迭代证据收集过程，强化学习能够有效训练AI执行这一复杂的医疗决策任务，为AI在临床治疗决策中的应用提供了新的技术路径。
