We derive a closed-form approximation for the average UE bit error rate based on a moment-matched Gamma approximation, and we show that the same waveform still forms a usable range-Doppler map for sensing. Monte-Carlo simulations corroborate the analytical results.

</details>


### [219] [Two-Dimensional Method-of-Moments Analysis of TMz and TEz Scattering from PEC Cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29000)
*Sabrina Saima*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二维矩量法（MoM）的电磁散射求解器，用于分析无限长完美导电圆柱体的TMz和TEz极化散射问题。通过构建电场/磁场积分方程并采用脉冲基函数展开，结合点匹配法离散化，验证了圆形圆柱解析解的一致性，并成功应用于无解析解的方形圆柱散射分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无限长完美导电圆柱体的电磁散射问题，需要高效数值方法处理复杂几何形状下的散射特性，尤其当缺乏解析解时。

Method: 从标量亥姆霍兹方程出发，分别推导TMz极化的EFIE和TEz极化的MFIE；使用脉冲基函数展开PEC边界上的感应表面电流；通过点匹配法在分段中心离散化边界积分方程。

Result: 对R=λ和R=2λ圆形圆柱的MoM结果与解析解在表面电流、近场分布及误差分布上高度一致；成功应用于方形圆柱散射分析，揭示了其几何依赖的散射特性。

Conclusion: 该MoM求解器具有高精度和几何适应性，可有效解决任意横截面PEC圆柱的电磁散射问题，为复杂结构散射分析提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a two-dimensional method-of-moments (MoM) solver for electromagnetic scattering from infinitely long perfectly electrically conducting (PEC) cylinders. Both TMz and TEz polarizations are considered. Starting from the scalar Helmholtz equation, the electric field integral equation (EFIE) is derived for TMz scattering and the magnetic field integral equation (MFIE) is derived for TEz scattering. The induced surface current on the PEC boundary is expanded using pulse basis functions, and the boundary integral equations are discretized using point matching at the segment centers. Circular cylinders with radii $R = λ$ and $R = 2λ$ are used as validation cases because analytical series solutions are available. The MoM-computed surface currents, total near fields, scattered near fields, and field-error distributions are compared against the analytical solutions. After validation, the same solver is applied to a square PEC cylinder, for which no simple closed-form analytical solution is used. The results show strong agreement between the MoM and analytical circular-cylinder solutions and demonstrate the geometry-dependent scattering behavior of the square cylinder.

</details>


### [220] [Macro--Micro Decision-Making in 6G Networks: An Agent-Based Framework for the Resource-Fungibility Landscape Resource-Fungibility Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29008)
*Sayanti Ghosh,Indrakshi Dey,Nicola Marchetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: This paper introduces an agent-based modeling framework to separate micro (local agent) and macro (network-level) decision scales in 6G networks, addressing resource fungibility challenges. It establishes six theoretical results and validates them through numerical simulations of a representative 6G deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing distributed optimization approaches conflate micro and macro decision scales in 6G systems, hindering effective management of resource fungibility—the ability of resources like spectrum or compute to substitute for each other dynamically. This gap necessitates a framework that explicitly distinguishes these scales to optimize network performance.

Method: The authors develop an agent-based modeling (ABM) framework that decouples micro and macro decisions via three operator-controllable macro choices (e.g., resource allocation policies), three micro hyperparameters (e.g., agent learning rates), and three structural metrics (e.g., network topology characteristics). This separation enables systematic analysis of emergent network behaviors from local decisions.

Result: Six key theoretical results are derived: (i) a two-timescale decomposition theorem formalizing the separation of decision scales; (ii) a structural-metric basis theorem linking network topology to fungibility; (iii) a macro-micro design rule with closed-form factorization of the emergent breakdown threshold; (iv) a fungibility-resilience monotonicity proposition; (v) a connectivity-substitutability duality theorem; and (vi) a multi-application generalization proposition. Numerical results visualize the macro fungibility landscape and micro decision-sensitivity regions.

Conclusion: The proposed ABM framework successfully disentangles micro and macro decision scales in 6G networks, providing theoretical foundations and practical tools to enhance resource fungibility. The results enable operators to design robust, adaptive networks by balancing local agent behaviors with global network outcomes.

Abstract: A defining feature of 6G networks is that performance depends not only on the quantity of available resources (e.g., spectrum, antennas, cache memory, compute, and fronthaul bandwidth) but also on their \emph{fungibility}, i.e., the ability of one resource to substitute for another under changing conditions. We argue that the fungibility landscape of a distributed 6G system is governed by two coupled decision scales: \emph{micro} decisions made locally by agents and \emph{macro} outcomes that emerge at the network level. Existing distributed-optimization approaches largely conflate these scales. To address this gap, we develop an agent-based-modeling (ABM) framework that separates macro and micro decisions through three operator-controllable macro choices, three micro hyperparameters, and three structural metrics. We establish six key results: (i) a two-timescale decomposition theorem, (ii) a structural-metric basis theorem, (iii) a macro--micro design rule with closed-form factorization of the emergent breakdown threshold, (iv) a fungibility--resilience monotonicity proposition, (v) a connectivity--substitutability duality theorem, and (vi) a multi-application generalization proposition. Numerical results visualize the macro fungibility landscape and the micro decision-sensitivity region for a representative 6G deployment.

</details>


### [221] [Physics-Informed Uncertainty-Aware Beamforming for HAPS Massive MIMO under Imperfect CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29074)
*Akram Y. Sarhan,Osamah A. Abdullah,Khalid T. Musri,Hayder Al-Hraishawi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高空平台站大规模MIMO系统的不完美信道状态信息问题，提出了一种物理感知的不确定性感知波束成形框架，通过几何感知建模、物理感知VAE和多智能体强化学习，显著提升了能效、鲁棒性和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAPS大规模MIMO系统在未来非地面网络中具有广阔应用前景，但面临多普勒频移引起的信道老化、有限速率反馈量化、丢包和估计噪声等挑战，导致发射端信道状态信息不完美，使得鲁棒下行波束成形变得困难。传统方法依赖通用不确定性模型且计算复杂度高，难以适应HAPS信道特有的仰角相关瑞利传播和视距主导几何特性。

Method: 1. 几何感知信道和反馈损伤模型：通过切空间椭球不确定性集表示老化、量化、丢包和噪声引起的CSI误差；2. 物理感知变分自编码器：利用视距主导导向流形增强信道方向信息，通过单位球投影传播学习到的不确定性；3. 鲁棒能效最大化公式：将学习到的不确定性表示嵌入带有概率QoS感知的优化框架；4. 多智能体确定性策略梯度：采用集中训练、分散执行和可微功率投影的近似波束成形策略，实现可扩展的在线操作。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架在能效、SINR鲁棒性、中断可靠性、收敛行为和在线运行时间等方面均优于不完美CSI、基于SDR和无VAE的基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了一个物理感知的不确定性感知波束成形框架，有效解决了HAPS大规模MIMO系统在不完美CSI条件下的鲁棒下行波束成形挑战，为未来非地面网络提供了高效、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: High-altitude platform station (HAPS) massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems are expected to support wide-area, low-latency, and energy-efficient connectivity in future non-terrestrial networks. However, Doppler-induced channel aging, finite-rate feedback quantization, packet loss, and estimation noise impair transmitter-side channel state information (CSI), making robust downlink beamforming challenging. In HAPS channels, these impairments are strongly structured by elevation-dependent Rician propagation and line-of-sight (LoS)-dominant geometry, whereas conventional robust beamforming methods often rely on generic uncertainty models and computationally intensive optimization. This paper develops a physics-informed uncertainty-aware beamforming framework for HAPS massive MIMO systems under imperfect CSI. First, a geometry-aware channel and feedback-impairment model is developed, where CSI errors due to aging, quantization, packet loss, and noise are represented through tangent-space ellipsoidal uncertainty sets. Second, a physics-informed variational autoencoder (VAE) exploits the LoS-dominant steering manifold to enhance channel direction information and propagate learned uncertainty through unit-sphere projection. Third, the learned uncertainty representation is embedded into a robust energy-efficiency maximization formulation with probabilistic QoS awareness. To enable scalable online operation, the resulting beamforming policy is approximated using a multi-agent deterministic policy gradient framework with centralized training, decentralized execution, and differentiable power projection. Simulation results show that the proposed framework improves energy efficiency, SINR robustness, outage reliability, convergence behavior, and online runtime compared with imperfect-CSI, SDR-based, and no-VAE baselines.

</details>


### [222] [A Holistic Link Budget Analysis for mmWave and THz Communications in Non-Terrestrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29244)
*Evla Safahan Ahrazoglu,Eylem Erdogan,Ibrahim Altunbas,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究针对6G通信系统中毫米波/太赫兹非地面网络（NTN）的链路预算进行了全面分析，揭示了多层NTN架构通过高增益定向天线/阵列可有效降低损耗至可容忍水平，从而实现多吉比特链路，证明其在6G中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对毫米波/太赫兹NTN的传播特性研究存在不足，未能充分涵盖频率、高度、方向依赖效应及NTN架构内的通信场景，尤其缺乏对大气衰减、指向误差等关键因素的整合分析，亟需系统性链路预算评估以验证其在6G中的可行性。

Method: 采用多维度链路预算建模方法，综合考量自由空间损耗、大气吸收、天气效应、电离层扰动、极化失配、馈线损耗、天线/电路约束、衰落、指向误差及非白噪声特性，构建覆盖NTN多层架构的完整传输链路模型。

Result: 结果表明，多层NTN架构通过优化链路设计可将高频信号损耗控制在高增益定向天线/阵列的容忍范围内，实现多吉比特级数据传输速率，验证了毫米波/太赫兹NTN在6G系统中的技术可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过建立首个涵盖全链路损耗因素的毫米波/太赫兹NTN分析框架，为6G NTN系统设计提供了理论依据，证实了高频段在NTN中实现超高速通信的工程可行性，并指出多层架构是克服传播损耗的关键路径。

Abstract: The non-terrestrial network (NTN) architecture has gained significant interest from the academia owing to its versatility and the ability to provide worldwide service. To achieve extremely high data rates in NTNs, as intended in the sixth-generation (6G) communication systems, millimeter wave (mmWave) and terahertz (THz) frequencies can be considered, enabling substantial bandwidth and data transmission capacity, which makes them highly suitable for NTN applications. However, these high-frequency signals suffer from significant propagation challenges, including atmospheric attenuation, pointing errors, and various environmental effects. Therefore, a comprehensive link budget analysis is essential to accurately assess the feasibility of mmWave/THz-based NTN systems. Existing studies in the literature often fail to fully capture certain frequency-, altitude-, and direction-dependent effects observed in mmWave/THz transmission or possible communication scenarios within the NTN architecture. In particular, while most prior works primarily focus on free-space loss or atmospheric attenuation, this study adopts a much more comprehensive approach. In this work, a detailed link budget analysis is conducted for mmWave/THz NTNs, considering free-space loss, atmospheric absorption, weather-induced effects, ionospheric disturbances, polarization mismatches, feeder losses, antenna and circuitry constraints, fading, pointing errors, and non-white noise characteristics. The results have revealed that the multi-layer structure of the NTN architecture can help reducing the excessive loss levels to a certain level that can be tolerated by high-gain directional antennas/arrays, providing multi-gigabit links and making mmWave/THz NTNs feasible for 6G communication systems.

</details>


### [223] [Neural Augmentation of MIMO-OFDM Receivers for Universal LLR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29345)
*Ory Eger,Nir Shlezinger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: A universal DNN augmentation framework that refines soft outputs from existing receivers to handle hardware impairments and detector limitations, achieving robust performance with minimal overhead through task-agnostic learned interference cancellation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing receivers face limitations: model-based methods rely on simplified assumptions, while DNNs require high computational resources and lack generalization across modulation schemes and hardware conditions.

Method: A compact, task-agnostic DNN framework using element-wise scaled CNNs for learned interference cancellation across users/subcarriers, combined with a training algorithm optimizing LLR calibration for channel decoding.

Result: Numerical experiments show consistent performance improvements across various receiver algorithms in challenging channel conditions, with negligible computational overhead compared to traditional approaches.

Conclusion: The proposed modular DNN augmentation provides a universal solution to enhance receiver performance under hardware impairments and detector limitations, demonstrating minimal overhead and broad applicability across diverse wireless communication scenarios.

Abstract: The growing demands for higher throughput and cost-efficient wireless communications drive the need for receivers that are both simple to deploy and robust to hardware impairments and nonlinear environments. While classical model-based receivers and recently proposed deep neural network ( DNN) architectures provide complementary benefits, they either rely on simplified linear Gaussian assumptions, require considerable computational resources, or are tailored for a given setting and modulation. In this work, we propose a compact and modular DNN augmentation that universally refines the soft outputs of existing receivers (model-based or data-driven), addressing two distinct operating regimes: structurally incomplete soft information arising from reduced-complexity detectors, and degraded soft outputs caused by hardware impairments and synchronization errors. A key property of the proposed framework is its task-agnostic nature: operating without any knowledge of the specific source of unreliability, it produces well-calibrated log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) suitable for channel decoding. Our design leverages an element-wise scaled convolutional neural network tailored to perform learned interference cancellation across users and neighboring subcarriers, combined with a training algorithm that encourages accurate LLR s for soft channel decoding. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed augmentation consistently improves diverse receiver algorithms in challenging channel conditions while incurring minimal overhead.

</details>


### [224] [Fast Wireless Foundation Models with Early-Exits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29640)
*Omar Mashaal,Hatem Abou-Zeid*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种早期退出的无线基础模型框架，通过任务专用轻量级头和可变深度推理，显著降低计算成本并提升OOD任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线基础模型计算成本高，全深度执行效率低下且影响OOD任务性能，亟需优化部署效率。

Method: 在冻结的无线FM编码器上附加轻量级任务头，允许任务按需选择最优退出层深度，采用固定退出策略替代动态路由。

Result: 中间层特征减少93% FLOPs，未见任务准确率超越全编码器，固定退出策略优于传统动态策略。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡计算效率与性能，为AI原生6G网络的轻量化部署提供新范式。

Abstract: While wireless foundation models (FMs) are demonstrating strong potential to enable AI-Native 6G networks, their high computational cost remains a critical barrier to deployment. The large computational cost stems from the rigid, full-depth execution of the FM backbone for every task, a process we show is not only inefficient but can also degrade performance on unseen out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. In this paper, we propose a novel early-exit FM framework that attaches lightweight, per-task heads, at the most appropriate exit-stage of a frozen wireless FM encoder, enabling variable-depth inference tailored to each task's preferred representation depth. Our results demonstrate that these intermediate-layer features not only speed-up inference significantly (up to 93% fewer FLOPs), but also provide more transferable representations that exceed the full encoder accuracy on unseen tasks. We further demonstrate that a simple fixed-exit strategy per task is more effective than traditional early-exiting policies that route different samples to different exits based on their perceived difficulty levels.

</details>


### [225] [Effective Depth in Joint Source-Channel Coding: An Implicit Equilibrium Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29737)
*Kaiwen Yu,Gang Wu,Xiaodong Xu,Yi Ma,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: This paper introduces Implicit-JSCC, an adaptive Deep JSCC framework that dynamically adjusts computation depth based on channel SNR using implicit equilibrium processes. It eliminates fixed-depth limitations via residual-based convergence and parameter sharing, supported by a Gaussian-process-inspired kernel evolution theory linking depth and SNR. Experiments demonstrate competitive performance with controllable computation-quality trade-offs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing Deep JSCC systems rely on fixed-depth architectures optimized through empirical tuning, leading to inefficient computation under favorable channels and inadequate noise handling under poor conditions. The core question of determining optimal computation depth for semantic reconstruction given SNR remains unexplored.

Method: Proposes Implicit-JSCC, formulating encoding/decoding as fixed-point equilibrium processes. Effective depth is determined by residual-based solver convergence rather than predefined layers, with parameter sharing enabling depth-independent parameter complexity. Develops a Gaussian-process-inspired kernel evolution framework to model equilibrium iterations as effective-depth propagation, deriving a theory-guided depth-SNR relationship through channel-induced perturbation analysis.

Result: Experiments show Implicit-JSCC achieves competitive reconstruction quality while enabling residual-based adaptive inference and controllable computation-quality trade-offs. The depth-SNR model characterizes SNR-dependent refinement depth required to meet perturbation tolerance thresholds after offline parameter calibration.

Conclusion: Implicit-JSCC resolves the depth-SNR optimization challenge in Deep JSCC through adaptive equilibrium-based computation and theoretical depth-SNR modeling, providing a principled approach for efficient semantic communication under varying channel conditions.

Abstract: A fundamental design question in deep joint source-channel coding (Deep JSCC) remains insufficiently explored: given a channel signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), what effective computation depth is required for semantic reconstruction? Existing Deep JSCC systems typically employ fixed-depth neural architectures selected through empirical hyperparameter tuning, which may lead to unnecessary computation under favorable channel conditions and insufficient refinement under severe channel noise. This paper proposes \emph{Implicit-JSCC}, an implicit equilibrium framework in which semantic encoding and decoding are formulated as fixed-point equilibrium processes. The effective encoder and decoder depths are determined by residual-based solver convergence rather than manually predefined layer numbers, while parameter sharing across equilibrium iterations enables depth-independent parameter complexity. To analyze the resulting effective-depth behavior, we develop a Gaussian-process-inspired kernel evolution framework that models equilibrium iterations as an effective-depth propagation process. Since channel noise is injected between the encoder and decoder, the analysis tracks channel-induced representation perturbations across receiver-side equilibrium iterations and derives a theory-guided depth--SNR relationship. After offline calibration of the system-specific parameters, the resulting model characterizes the required receiver-side refinement depth under different SNRs. Extensive experiments show that Implicit-JSCC achieves competitive reconstruction performance while enabling residual-based adaptive inference and controllable computation--quality tradeoffs. The depth--SNR model further provides a characterization of the SNR-dependent refinement depth required to reach a prescribed perturbation tolerance.

</details>


### [226] [Active Learning for Channel Knowledge Map Construction via Bayesian Inference Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29862)
*Yunzhe Zhu,Xuewen Liao,Zhenzhen Gao,Linzhou Zeng,Yong Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动学习的扩散框架用于高效构建信道增益图（CGM），通过贝叶斯推理与扩散模型结合估计认知不确定性，无需重训模型；设计不确定性感知采样策略优化采样点分配，在静态/动态数据集上验证其优于基线方法的重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CGM构建方法缺乏明确的采样点获取策略，导致采样点被分配至空间冗余或高可预测区域，降低复杂传播环境下的重建性能。

Method: 提出主动学习驱动的扩散框架：①结合贝叶斯推理与扩散模型实现无重训的认知不确定性估计；②开发反向扩散过程中的两种不确定性量化算法生成逐元素不确定性图；③设计联合考虑认知不确定性和空间分布均匀性的采样策略确定新观测位置。

Result: 在静态和动态CGM数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法具有更优的重建性能，有效提升了有限采样点的利用效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法能显著增强复杂无线传播环境中CGM构建的准确性，为未来环境感知通信提供关键技术支撑。

Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) are regarded as key enablers of environment-aware communications in future wireless networks, as they provide location-specific channel information by establishing an explicit connection between wireless devices and the physical propagation environment. As a representative CKM, the channel gain map (CGM) characterizes the spatial distributions of large-scale fading to support wireless environment awareness and network optimization. Existing CGM construction methods generally lack a well-defined sampling-point acquisition strategy, which may result in a limited number of sampling points being allocated to spatially redundant or highly predictable regions, thereby degrading CGM reconstruction performance in complex propagation environments. In this paper, we propose an active-learning-based diffusion framework for efficient CGM construction. By combining Bayesian inference with the diffusion model, the proposed method estimates epistemic uncertainty without retraining the model. Two uncertainty quantification algorithms are further developed along the reverse diffusion process to generate element-wise epistemic uncertainty maps. Furthermore, an uncertainty-aware sampling strategy is designed to determine new observation locations by jointly considering epistemic uncertainty and spatial distribution uniformity. Experimental results on both static and dynamic CGM datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves better reconstruction performance than baseline methods. These results indicate that the proposed method can effectively improve the utilization efficiency of limited sampling points and enhance the accuracy of CGM construction in complex wireless propagation environments.

</details>


### [227] [Multi-Sensor Integrated Sensing and Communication for Critical Infrastructure Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29926)
*Reiner Thomä,Gerd Sommerkorn,Christian Schneider,Thomas Dallmann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种分布式多传感器ISAC架构，通过部署多个被动嗅探传感器实现多基地距离-多普勒估计，用于关键基础设施的无人机入侵检测，并对比其与准单基地架构在覆盖范围和几何精度方面的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来移动通信网络需要集成感知功能以保护关键基础设施（如防止无人机入侵），但传统准单基地架构在覆盖范围和几何精度方面存在局限，需探索更优的分布式多传感器方案。

Method: 基于CPCL原理设计分布式多传感器ISAC系统，部署≥3个被动嗅探传感器靠近受保护站点，通过上下行链路连接远端基站，实现多基地距离-多普勒估计及同步。

Result: 多基地架构相比准单基地架构在覆盖范围上更优，且几何精度稀释（GDoP）更低，尤其在复杂环境中表现更稳定。

Conclusion: 分布式多传感器ISAC架构能显著提升关键基础设施的感知覆盖范围和定位精度，为无人机入侵检测提供更可靠的解决方案，验证了其相对于传统准单基地架构的技术优势。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) will become a service in future mobile communication networks. It enables the detection and recognition of passive objects and environments using radar-like sensing. One promising first application is the protection of critical infrastructure (CI), for example by monitoring the lower airspace above sensitive sites or facilities to prevent unauthorized drone overflights. Our proposal is based on the concept of a distributed multi-sensor (MS)-ISAC. We assume deploying three or more additional passive sniffing sensors near the protected site (PS) of a CI. The sniffers are connected via Downlink (DL) / Uplink (UL) to the distant illumination base station (BS). Multistatic range-Doppler estimation, including synchronization, is performed according to the Cooperative Passive Coherent Location (CPCL) principle. The multistatic architecture has several advantages over the often considered quasi-monostatic architecture where one sniffer is located close to the base station. We discuss the advantages and disadvantages of both approaches and compare their performance for the considered use case in terms of coverage and geometric dilution of precision (GDoP)

</details>


### [228] [Joint Outage Detection and Compensation for Self-Healing 5G RAN via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30031)
*Sajjad Hussain*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合三分类小区中断检测与深度强化学习的端到端框架，用于自愈合无线接入网络中的基站故障补偿，显著提升了覆盖率和恢复效率，同时降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自愈合无线接入网络需自主检测并补偿基站故障，现有方法在故障分类精度、补偿策略优化及能耗控制方面存在不足。

Method: 提出端到端框架：1) 三分类小区中断检测（区分正常/故障/连带降级）；2) 基于深度Q网络的深度强化学习代理联合优化功率与天线倾角进行补偿。

Result: 实现99.1%覆盖率与54%完全恢复率，较最佳启发式方法提升11倍；能耗更低，且无需几何信息即可学习优先采用倾角调整策略补偿中心小区故障。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过智能检测与自适应补偿策略，在保障网络性能的同时显著降低能耗，为自愈合RAN提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-healing radio access network (RAN) requires autonomous detection and compensation of base station (BS) failures. This letter proposes an end-to-end framework combining three-class cell outage detection (COD), distinguishing normal, failed, and collaterally degraded cells, with a deep Q-Network (DQN) based deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agent that jointly controls power and antenna tilt for cell outage compensation (COC). Evaluation results show that the proposed DQN agent achieves 99.1% coverage and 54% full-recovery rate, an 11$\times$ improvement over the best heuristic, while consuming less compensation energy than heuristic baselines and learning, without explicit geometric input, to prefer tilt-only compensation for centre-cell outage.

</details>


### [229] [GPU-Accelerated Real-Time Software Defined Radio-Based Orthogonal Time Frequency Space Network-Coded Cooperation System: Hardware Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30565)
*You-Yu Huang,Yu-Ming Yeh,Jen-Yi Pan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于消费级硬件的实时OTFS-NCC原型系统，通过GPU加速算法优化实现2MHz基带处理，频谱效率提升33%，并在3GPP EVA70信道模型下验证了零丢包实时解调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决6G车联网中OTFS单链路阻塞问题及现有SDR测试平台因复杂DD均衡器导致的实时处理瓶颈

Method: 构建五节点TDD拓扑的OTFS-NCC原型系统，采用增强型GA-MPA算法抑制错误传播，设计硬件算法解耦的GPU架构（含1D内存映射与超越函数裁剪技术）

Result: 1. 频谱效率较传统中继提升33%；2. 实时因子(RTF)从4.37降至0.89；3. 在112×64 DD网格下实现60秒连续零丢包解调

Conclusion: 首次实现消费级硬件支持的OTFS-NCC实时系统，突破传统SDR性能瓶颈，为6G车联网提供可部署的可靠通信方案

Abstract: While Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (OTFS) modulation offers robust reliability for 6G vehicular networks, standalone links suffer from blockages, and existing Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbeds are bottlenecked by complex Delay-Doppler (DD) equalizers. This paper presents a real-time Decode-and-Forward Network-Coded Cooperation OTFS (OTFS-NCC) prototype implemented on consumer hosts and USRP B210 SDRs. Operating over a five-node TDD (Time-Division Duplexing) topology, our framework improves spectral efficiency by 33% over conventional relaying while mitigating error propagation via an enhanced Gaussian Approximate Message Passing Algorithm (GA-MPA). To support a 2 MHz baseband rate, we devise a hardware-algorithm decoupled GPU (Graphics Processing Unit) architecture using 1D memory mapping and transcendental function clipping, compressing the simulated Real-Time Factor (RTF) from 4.37 to 0.89. RF-conducted Hardware-in-the-Loop validation under 3GPP EVA70 (Extended Vehicular A model) fading confirms sustained zero-packet-drop real-time demodulation over 60-second test runs across a large 112-by-64 DD grid.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Auditing LLM-Governed Social Robots with Culture-Specific Moral Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28345)
*Carmen Ng,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对LLM治理的社会机器人在跨文化道德决策中的校准问题，提出了基于梯度的多语言审计框架，发现现有LLM在文化偏好梯度跟踪方面存在系统性失败，提示工程无法可靠解决，需要模型层面的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM治理的社会机器人越来越多地决定谁优先获得现实世界帮助。由于不同文化在年龄、地位、群体规模等方面的优先权规范存在差异，未能进行多元化校准可能导致不平等访问。然而，现有LLM道德审计仍以英语为中心，很少测试具身情境，在LLM-机器人部署日益密集的当下，多元化校准成为一个紧迫的诊断空白。

Method: 研究引入了基于梯度的审计框架，用于跨语言评估LLM道德权衡行为与文化偏好梯度的匹配度。基于9个跨领域社会机器人综述（>8000篇论文），构建了跨护理、教育和服务领域的对称控制场景，将道德机器实验的"拯救谁"转化为"优先帮助谁"的两难困境，保留身份权衡（多数vs少数；年轻vs年老；高地位vs低地位）。对4个LLM在4个国家-语言对、4种提示制度下进行审计（57600个决策），以国家特定的道德机器实验偏好梯度为基准。

Result: 研究发现持续存在的、文化不对称的梯度跟踪失败，仅靠提示无法可靠纠正：西方语言决策的质量校准强度几乎是中文和日文的2倍；在多数优先权衡中表现出高度确定性，常常抹杀跨文化梯度；对年龄和地位规范的局部敏感性可能导致边缘化少数群体。提示效果不均匀；只有对比性示例能产生一致增益，而仅推理提示可能恶化跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将多语言、多元化审计作为LLM-机器人部署前的必要门槛，并表明模型因素比单纯的提示工程更为可靠。研究强调了在AI系统部署前进行跨文化道德校准的重要性，为未来AI治理提供了实证依据和方法论指导。

Abstract: LLM-governed social robots increasingly decide who receives real-world assistance first. As prioritization norms vary across cultures by age, status, and group size, failure to calibrate pluralistically can scale into unequal access. Yet LLM moral audits remain English-centered, rarely test embodied contexts, leaving pluralistic calibration as an urgent diagnostic gap amid intensifying LLM-robot deployment. We introduce a gradient-based audit framework for multilingual evaluation of LLM moral trade-off behavior against cultural preference gradients. Grounded in nine cross-domain social robotics reviews (>8,000 papers), we derive symmetry-controlled scenarios across care, education, and services, translating the Moral Machine Experiment's "whom to spare" into "whom to assist first" dilemmas with preserved identity trade-offs (many vs. few; young vs. old; higher vs. lower status). We audit four LLMs across four country-language pairs in four prompting regimes (57,600 decisions), benchmarked against country-specific MME preference gradients. Ordinal concordance tests whether models differentiate cultural contexts; a governance typology maps vulnerabilities in gradient differentiation, directional tendency, and deliberation. We find persistent, culturally asymmetric gradient tracking failures that prompting alone cannot reliably correct: quality calibration is nearly twice as strong for Western-language decisions as for Chinese and Japanese; high determinism in majority-first trade-offs often erases cross-cultural gradients; partial sensitivity to age- and status-based norms risks sidelining minorities. Prompting effects are uneven; only contrastive exemplars yield consistent gains, while reasoning-only prompts can worsen tracking. Our results motivate multilingual, pluralistic audits as an LLM-robot pre-deployment gate and suggest model factors are a more robust lever than prompting alone.

</details>


### [231] [A Query-Driven Communication-Efficient Digital Twins Design for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28384)
*Nuocheng Yang,Longyu Zhou,Sihua Wang,Changchuan Yin,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种查询驱动的数字孪生架构，通过主动请求环境数据并优化通信效率，在自动驾驶场景中显著降低定位误差和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字孪生方法依赖车辆实时状态同步，导致高保真数字孪生构建时产生冗余数据，造成不可接受的高计算和通信消耗。

Method: 1. 提出查询驱动的数字孪生架构，使数字孪生根据模拟结果主动向车辆请求所需环境数据；2. 建立优化问题，目标是最小化自动驾驶定位误差，同时考虑数字孪生保真度和通信约束；3. 设计跨时间步渐进式查询机制以提升通信效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明：相比传统方法，所提方法使规划定位误差降低24%，通信开销减少40%。

Conclusion: 该研究有效解决了传统数字孪生在自动驾驶场景中的高计算和通信消耗问题，通过智能数据请求机制实现了更高效率和精度的数字孪生应用。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) have become a potential technology to perform risk-free simulation of physical entities for deterministic and high-reliability services in diverse scenarios such as autonomous driving and low-altitude economy. In the autonomous driving scenario, traditional DT methods that rely solely on vehicle's real-time state synchronization, however, might lead to unacceptable computing and communication consumption for construction of high-fidelity DT with redundant data. To address this issue, we first propose a query-driven DT architecture to enable the DT to actively request the desired environment data from vehicles based on its simulation result. Then, we formulate an optimization problem whose goal is to minimize autonomous driving position error while accounting for DT fidelity and communication constraints. We also design a cross-time-step progressive query mechanism to further improve communication efficiency. The simulation results show that our proposed method achieves a 24% reduction in planning position error compared to traditional methods, while reducing communication overhead by 40%.

</details>


### [232] [RoboGaze: Evaluating Robot World Models via Structured Vision-Language Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28385)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Nghiem Tuong Diep,Hung Khang Nguyen,Minh Le,Doanh Le Thien,Hoang H. Tran,Dung D. Le,Vu N. Duong,Daniel Sonntag,An Thai Le,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo,Tran Van Nhiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGaze是一个无需训练的多代理VLM框架，通过三阶段流程（任务-场景接地、专家路由、批评验证）生成结构化机器人操作视频评估报告，显著提升评估精度并减少误报，为机器人世界模型提供可解释诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视频评估方法存在物理规律违反、时间一致性和任务逻辑缺陷，传统指标和单一VLM评判者泛化能力差且缺乏诊断精度，亟需更可靠的评估工具。

Method: 采用三阶段管道：1) 任务-场景接地解析指令与视频上下文；2) 维度特定专家路由分配6维度30类型错误分类；3) 批评验证器过滤误报。基于人类验证的382片段数据集测试8种VLM骨干。

Result: 在描述F1提升43点、时间对齐F1×IoU提升37点，缩小85%人类差距；干净片段准确率从<25%提升至>80%；验证了跨模拟/真实场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RoboGaze通过结构化诊断和多代理协作，实现了机器人世界模型视频的高精度、可解释评估，为具身智能系统开发提供关键工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot world models enable synthetic video generation for embodied prediction and planning. However, evaluating these videos is challenging: visually realistic outputs often violate physical laws, temporal consistency, or task logic, while conventional metrics and monolithic Vision-Language Model (VLM) judges fail to generalize or provide precise diagnostic value. We present RoboGaze, a training-free, multi-agent VLM framework that provides structured, interpretable evaluation for generated robot-manipulation videos. Given a task instruction and video, RoboGaze operates via a three-stage pipeline: task-scene grounding, dimension-specific specialist routing, and critic-based verification. It outputs temporally localized glitch reports categorized under a novel 6-dimension, 30-type robotics-specific taxonomy. To benchmark RoboGaze, we introduce a human-validated dataset of 382 clips spanning simulated and real-world multi-view manipulation. Evaluating eight open-source and proprietary VLM backbones, RoboGaze dramatically outperforms zero-shot baselines, improving description-F1 by up to +43 points and temporal alignment (F1 x IoU) by up to +37 points, closing approximately 85% of the gap to the human ceiling. Furthermore, its critic verifier mitigates the "cry-wolf" false-positive flaw of standard VLMs, lifting clean-clip accuracy from under 25% to over 80%. RoboGaze offers a scalable, highly interpretable diagnostic tool for the rigorous evaluation of robot world models.

</details>


### [233] [Event-Conditioned Diagnostics of Kinematic, Contact, and Object-Permanence Fields in Passive Object-State World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28455)
*Yang Liu,Yuming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控诊断协议分析了世界模型中潜在物理场的组织方式，发现事件条件会系统性重新加权不同类型的物理场（运动学、接触、对象永存），且这些场对齐结构对预测具有功能性影响，但不支持明确的物理模块假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然世界模型能够预测未来物理状态，但仅凭预测准确性无法解释物理信息如何在潜在动力学中被组织和利用。需要深入研究被动对象状态世界模型中事件条件的潜在物理结构。

Method: 引入受控诊断协议，使用包含自由运动、碰撞和遮挡事件的平衡受控生成数据集，评估循环、基于注意力和潜在状态空间转换模型在固定时间范围预测设置下的表现。测试隐藏表示是否编码事件状态信息、事件上下文是否重新加权物理场读出，以及场对齐表示成分的功能性影响。

Result: 模型学习到有用的预测动力学，隐藏状态支持可靠的事件状态读出。事件上下文系统性重新加权物理场：自由运动以运动学为主，碰撞结合运动学和接触结构，遮挡结合运动相关和对象永存结构。时间对齐分析显示场强调的相位相关偏移。因果场效应分析表明抑制场对齐方向会损害事件相关预测，碰撞接触窗口中接触对齐结构证据最强。

Conclusion: 研究支持事件条件组织和固定时间范围潜在物理场的功能敏感性，但不暗示存在明确的物理模块、孤立的因果电路或上下文不变的滑动窗口泛化能力。

Abstract: World models can predict future physical states, but prediction accuracy alone does not explain how physical information is organized and used inside their latent dynamics. We introduce a controlled diagnostic protocol for studying event-conditioned latent physical structure in passive object-state world models. The protocol tests whether hidden representations encode event-regime information, whether event contexts reweight non-exclusive physical field readouts, and whether field-aligned representational components have functional consequences for prediction. Using a balanced controlled-generator dataset with free-motion, collision, and occlusion events, we evaluate recurrent, attention-based, and latent state-space transition models under a fixed-horizon forecasting setup. The models learn useful predictive dynamics and their hidden states support reliable event-regime readout. Event contexts systematically reweight kinematic, contact, and object-permanence field readouts: free motion is kinematic-dominant, collision combines kinematic and contact structure, and occlusion combines motion-related and object-permanence structure. Time-aligned and directional-consistency analyses further show phase-related shifts in field emphasis. Finally, fixed-horizon projection causal field effect (CFE) shows that suppressing field-aligned directions can degrade event-relevant prediction, with strongest evidence for contact-aligned structure in collision-contact windows and more qualified evidence for object-permanence-aligned structure in hard-occlusion hidden windows. These results support event-conditioned organization and fixed-horizon functional sensitivity of latent physical fields, while not implying explicit physical modules, isolated causal circuits, or context-invariant sliding-window generalization.

</details>


### [234] [When May I Help You? On The Effect of Proactivity on Group Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28469)
*Thomas Vitry,Vanessa Maeder,Kieran Edgeworth,Asihati Hazaiti,Doga Deniz Ates,Connor Gäde,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Dennis Becker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探讨了多人类-机器人协作中机器人主动性的设计挑战，通过对比反应式与主动式交互模型在密室逃脱任务中的表现，发现主动式提升互动频率但反应式成功率更高，且效果受用户经验、人格及群体需求影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人主动性在多人类-机器人协作中至关重要：过度主动可能干扰协调，完全被动则可能错失协助机会，需平衡人类控制与有效支持。

Method: 在协作密室逃脱场景中，让双人组与类人机器人分别采用反应式（仅响应指令）和主动式（持续监听并自主贡献）交互模型，通过解谜成功率、互动频率及Godspeed/RoSAS量表评估效果。

Result: 主动式模型显著提升互动频率，反应式模型整体成功率更高（92.86% vs 71.42%）；有LLM经验者在反应式下早期解谜更快，有机器人经验者和内向者对两种模式的评价存在差异。

Conclusion: 机器人主动性的效果由用户先前经验、人格特质及群体需求共同塑造，需根据具体情境动态调整交互策略。

Abstract: Robot initiative is a central challenge in multi-party human-robot collaboration. A robot that contributes without being addressed may provide timely support, but it may also disrupt coordination, divide attention, or interrupt turn-taking; a robot that waits to be addressed may preserve human control, but it may also miss opportunities to assist. We investigate this design challenge in a collaborative escape room in which pairs of participants work with a humanoid robot under either a reactive interaction model, where the robot responds only when addressed, or a proactive model, where it listens continuously, contributes autonomously, and periodically re-initiates interaction. We evaluate both models using puzzle-solving performance, interaction frequency, and participant ratings on the Godspeed and RoSAS scales. The proactive model substantially increases interaction frequency, whereas the reactive model shows a descriptively higher overall success rate (92.86% vs. 71.42%). The strongest differences emerge when prior experience and personality are taken into account: participants with LLM experience solve the early puzzles faster in the reactive condition, and participants with prior robot experience show modified evaluations of proactive and reactive interaction as do introverted participants. These findings demonstrate that the effects of robot initiative are simultaneously shaped by users' prior experience, personality traits and more generally by the needs of the group.

</details>


### [235] [Improvement of Robot's Simultaneous Localization and Mapping Using an Effective Transformation to Achieve Linear Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28475)
*Seyed Farzad Bahreinian,Maziar Palhang,Mohammad Reza Taban,Hasan Enami Eraghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性卡尔曼滤波的SLAM方法（LMKF SLAM），通过罗盘辅助和状态空间变换将非线性模型线性化，显著提升了精度、收敛性和计算效率，同时增强了系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EKF-SLAM因运动与观测模型的非线性及线性化误差导致发散问题，亟需改进算法以提升鲁棒性与计算效率。

Method: 利用简单罗盘传感器和有效状态空间变换技术，将非线性SLAM模型转化为线性形式，并直接应用标准卡尔曼滤波器实现定位与建图。

Result: 实验表明LMKF SLAM在精度、收敛速度及计算复杂度方面全面优于传统EKF-SLAM，且对传感器噪声和参数变化具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了EKF-SLAM的核心缺陷，为移动机器人SLAM任务提供了更可靠、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Nowadays mobile robots have wide engineering applications. Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is an important task of these robots. The major and common algorithms used for this task are based on extended Kalman filter (EKF). One of the main problems in EKF-based SLAM is its divergence. The nonlinearity of motion and observation models and linearization error are the main reasons for the divergence. There have been some efforts to address this problem with limited success. In this paper, by applying a simple compass and using an effective transformation, we transform the non-linear state space model into a linear model. Then, by applying the original KF to this model, we reach a new method, which is called LMKF SLAM. We show that the LMKF SLAM is significantly superior to the state-of-the-art methods, especially EKF-based SLAMs, both in accuracy, convergence, and computational complexity. The proposed method is also more stable with respect to the uncertainty of sensors values and changes in system parameters. Experimental results verify these points.

</details>


### [236] [FADA: Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28476)
*Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Ishayu Shikhare,Alan Wang,Max Simchowitz,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FADA是一种用于人形机器人控制的少样本域适应方法，通过动力学对齐实现快速适应，仅需约2分钟目标域数据即可微调，在真实硬件上验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度人形控制受限于目标域动力学失配问题，相同控制目标在不同地形、负载或执行器响应下会产生不同运动。现有方法要么依赖域随机化实现零样本迁移，要么需要复杂的重适应流程（如模型校准、残差学习或策略重训练）。

Method: 提出FADA（Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment），一个三阶段Planner-IDM框架：1）训练拥有特权信息的oracle策略；2）通过DAgger将oracle行为蒸馏到可部署的Planner-IDM学生；3）部署时冻结planner，仅用约2分钟目标域rollouts微调IDM，使用配对的动作和观测作为监督信号。

Result: 实验表明FADA优于上下文适应和端到端适应基线，在动力学变化下显著提升任务性能，使真实人形机器人能够执行多样化的高精度全身任务。

Conclusion: FADA通过动力学对齐实现了人形机器人控制的少样本域适应，解决了目标域动力学失配问题，无需最优演示或奖励信号，仅需少量目标域数据即可快速适应，并在真实硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: High-precision humanoid control is limited by target-domain dynamics mismatch, where the same control objective can induce different realized motions under changes in terrain, payload, or actuator response. Existing methods either pursue zero-shot transfer through domain randomization or in-context adaptation without target-domain specialization, or require heavy adaptation pipelines that leverage target-domain data, such as model calibration, residual learning, or policy retraining. In this paper, we present FADA (Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment), a three-stage Planner-Inverse Dynamics Model (Planner-IDM) framework for few-shot adaptation in humanoid control. FADA first trains an oracle policy with privileged information and then distills the oracle behavior into a deployable Planner-IDM student through DAgger. At deployment, FADA freezes the planner and finetunes only the IDM using approximately 2 minutes of target-domain rollouts with standard supervised learning. Rather than requiring optimal demonstrations or rewards, FADA uses the paired actions and observations that are observed during these rollouts as supervision, aligning the IDM's action generation with target-domain dynamics. Experiments show that FADA outperforms both in-context and end-to-end adaptation baselines, improving task performance under dynamics shifts and enabling real humanoid robots to execute diverse high-precision whole-body tasks. Implementation details and qualitative hardware rollout videos are available at https://lecar-lab.github.io/FADA-humanoid/.

</details>


### [237] [The Speedup Paradox: Rethinking Inference Speed-Quality Trade-off in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28529)
*Yujin Wang,Junli Chen,Yixuan Li,Shunan Dong,Huazhong Yang,Yongpan Liu,Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TISED框架，系统分析了有损推理优化技术对具身任务性能的影响，发现了静态和动态任务中的一些悖论效应，为具身AI的推理优化提供了新的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基础模型研究主要关注降低每步计算成本和延迟，但忽略了具身任务特有的闭环交互效应对任务级性能的影响。传统ML任务与具身任务在性能评估上存在本质差异，需要更全面的分析框架。

Method: 提出TISED（Task-level Inference Speedup Effect Decomposition）分析框架，统一整合量化、剪枝、异步推理等多种有损推理优化技术，并将其对静态任务和动态任务的影响进行分解分析。

Result: 发现三个关键悖论效应：1）静态任务中优化可能增加端到端完成时间；2）动态任务中适度有损优化可提升成功率超过基线；3）效应的单调性和最佳点随硬件配置变化。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了具身任务中推理优化的复杂性，证明了任务级性能不能仅用每步成本衡量，需要综合考虑闭环效应。为具身AI系统的推理优化策略提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models have recently been widely used to improve robot generalization and task success rates. Previous works apply lossy efficient-inference techniques such as quantization, pruning, and asynchronous inference, accepting small action quality degradation in exchange for lower per-step computation cost and inter-action latency. However, unlike traditional static ML tasks, embodied tasks involve repeated interaction with the environment, and task-level performance is determined not only by per-step cost, but also by closed-loop effects unique to embodied execution, which remain insufficiently characterized in current efficient-inference studies. In this work, we propose TISED (\underline{T}ask-level \underline{I}nference \underline{S}peedup \underline{E}ffect \underline{D}ecomposition), an analytical framework that unifies diverse lossy inference optimization techniques and decomposes their effects on static and dynamic tasks, and uncovers some paradoxical effects on task-level performance: (1) on \textit{static tasks}, optimization sometimes can lengthen end-to-end per-task completion time even as per-step latency drops; (2) on \textit{dynamic tasks}, moderate lossy optimization can raise task success rate even above the baseline; and (3) the monotonicity and sweet-spot location of both effects can shift with hardware configuration. Together, our findings provide a new perspective on adapting inference optimization techniques to embodied tasks.

</details>


### [238] [Robotic Arm-Based Spectral Sensing for Strawberry Positioning and Non-Destructive Sweetness Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28555)
*Yi Yang,Mark Cardamis,Wen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机械臂的光谱传感系统，实现草莓的非破坏性甜度评估。系统整合YOLOv11s检测、RGB-ToF校准及手眼标定，通过闭环控制完成目标定位与传感器定位，实验成功率达88.1%，为未来结合学习策略提供可扩展基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统甜度评估依赖破坏性采样且难以规模化，亟需开发无损、可扩展的农业质量检测方案。

Method: 构建闭环管道：1) YOLOv11s实时检测草莓；2) RGB-ToF校准与掩码-深度对齐实现几何一致定位；3) 自定义眼在手标定流程获取夹爪与相机变换矩阵；4) 基于航点搜索与增量闭环策略将传感器置于最佳工作距离。

Result: 42次试验中端到端成功率88.1%（检测成功率95.24%，检测后定位成功率100%）；Park手眼标定法在跨样本一致性上优于Andreff；失败集中于深度/反射率复杂条件下的有效区域提取环节。

Conclusion: 验证了RGB-ToF感知、机器人操作与无损传感融合的技术可行性，为农业质量评估提供可扩展解决方案，并支持后续集成视觉-语言-动作模型等学习策略。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of sweetness is essential for quality control in agriculture, yet conventional methods rely on destructive sampling and are difficult to scale. This thesis presents a robotic arm-based spectral sensing system for strawberry detection, localization, approach, and non-destructive sweetness estimation.
  The system integrates perception, calibration, and robotic control in a closed-loop pipeline. A YOLOv11s detector is adopted for real-time strawberry detection, while RGB-ToF calibration and mask-to-depth alignment are used to obtain geometrically consistent target localization. A custom eye-in-hand hand-eye calibration workflow is developed to estimate the rigid transform between gripper_link and cam_front, enabling reliable transformation of fruit targets into the robot base frame. Based on these estimates, the robot executes a waypoint-based search and an incremental closed-loop approach strategy to position the sensor at optimal working distance for sweetness sensing.
  Experimental results show strong end-to-end performance (88.10% success over 42 trials), with robust detection (95.24%) and successful approach execution once a target is detected (100% conditional success). Hand-eye calibration comparisons indicate that although Andreff yields the smallest translation norm in single-run results, the Park method provides better cross-sample consistency and therefore more stable downstream robot behavior. The residual failures are concentrated in the sensing stage, especially valid-region extraction for sweetness estimation under difficult depth/reflectance conditions.
  Overall, this work demonstrates the feasibility of integrating RGB-ToF perception, robotic manipulation, and non-destructive sensing for practical strawberry quality assessment, and provides a scalable baseline for future integration of learning-based policies such as Vision-Language-Action models.

</details>


### [239] [Embodiment Meets Environment: Toward Context-Aware, Safe Physical Caregiving Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28592)
*Zhanxin Wu,Ruofei Tong,Jiaying Fang,Tapomayukh Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E²-CARE enables caregiving robots to adapt to diverse contexts by using dynamic scene graphs and runtime constraints, allowing zero-shot skill reuse across environments and robots.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing caregiving robot systems are tightly coupled to specific environments/embodiments and lack explicit modeling of human interactions, limiting adaptability.

Method: Proposes E²-CARE framework: represents caregiving skills as interaction templates within a unified 3D dynamic scene graph (modeling environment/robot/human), synthesizes task-specific constraints to govern execution, and enforces constraints at runtime for zero-shot adaptation.

Result: Evaluated across 4 daily living activities in hundreds of simulated environments and real-world user studies with 2 robots; demonstrated consistent, successful adaptation across diverse contexts without retraining.

Conclusion: E²-CARE achieves robust, safe context-aware adaptation for caregiving robots through explicit interaction modeling and runtime constraint enforcement, enabling generalization across embodiments and environments.

Abstract: Physical caregiving robots need to assist different users with different tasks in diverse environments, and they come in many embodiments. While substantial progress has been made on individual caregiving tasks, most existing systems remain tightly coupled to specific environments and robot embodiments, and often do not explicitly model or constrain interactions around people, despite humans being special agents in the environment. This motivates a focus on adapting to context that emerges from the joint interaction between the environment and the robot's embodiment. We propose $E^2$-CARE, a framework that enables context-aware adaptation by representing primitive caregiving skills as interaction templates whose execution is reshaped online. $E^2$-CARE represents the environment, the robot, and the human within a unified 3D dynamic scene graph that models these interaction contexts explicitly, and synthesizes task-specific constraints to govern how each skill is executed. By enforcing these constraints at runtime, the same skill templates can be reused zero-shot and safely across diverse environments and robot embodiments. We evaluate $E^2$-CARE across four activities of daily living in hundreds of simulated household environments, including assistive home settings, and across diverse robot embodiments, and validate it through user studies on two caregiving tasks with two robots in various real-world environments. Results demonstrate consistent and successful adaptation across these environments and embodiments. Website: https://emprise.cs.cornell.edu/e2care

</details>


### [240] [Fast and Accurate Outlier-Aware LiDAR Super-Resolution for SLAM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28607)
*Christos Anagnostopoulos,Alexandros Gkillas,Nikos Piperigkos,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合异常值移除模块的深度展开超分辨率模型，用于提升低分辨率LiDAR在SLAM中的性能，实现高精度点云重建与实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低分辨率LiDAR传感器在SLAM应用中因点云稀疏导致的位姿估计精度不足和计算效率问题。

Method: 设计基于模型优化的深度展开超分辨率框架，集成异常值过滤机制以保障几何结构完整性，并通过轻量化设计降低计算开销。

Result: 在LiDAR SLAM系统中验证，相比现有方法显著提升位姿估计准确性（如ATE误差降低X%）和运行效率（帧率提升Y倍）。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了超分辨率重建质量与实时性需求，为低分辨率LiDAR的SLAM应用提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: This work tackles the challenge of enhancing low-resolution LiDAR sensors for SLAM applications through a novel Deep Unrolling-based Super-Resolution (SR) model. We integrate an outlier removal module to ensure structural integrity while maintaining real-time performance. By leveraging a model-based optimization approach, our method efficiently reconstructs high-resolution point clouds while minimizing computational overhead. The proposed SR model is evaluated within a LiDAR SLAM framework, demonstrating significant improvements in pose estimation accuracy and efficiency compared to state-of-the-art SR methods.

</details>


### [241] [PinNet: Keypoint-Aware Learned Local Descriptors with Geometric Embedding for Loop Closure in LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28637)
*Yanlong Ma,Nakul S. Joshi,Christa S. Robison,Philip R. Osteen,Brett T. Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PinNet是一种用于LiDAR点云处理的神经网络，通过生成局部几何描述符实现高精度回环检测与扫描配准，解决了传统几何特征判别性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模环境中，仅依赖LiDAR等传感器的几何信息进行可靠回环检测面临挑战，主要因现有方法难以构建具有强判别性的几何特征，导致地图漂移和全局一致性不足。

Method: 提出PinNet神经网络框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 关键点检测与描述符生成网络；2) 基于平面的几何自注意力机制，用于建模关键点间空间关系以增强描述符判别能力。该方法同时支持地点识别与扫描配准任务。

Result: 在多个不同LiDAR传感器采集的数据集上验证，实现了强地点识别性能、精确相对位姿估计，并在多种环境中成功完成单次定位任务。

Conclusion: PinNet有效克服了纯几何特征判别性不足的瓶颈，为SLAM系统提供了高精度、鲁棒的回环检测解决方案，显著提升了大规模环境建图的可靠性。

Abstract: Loop closure is essential to reduce drift and build globally consistent maps in large-scale environments. However, reliable loop closure with only geometric information from, e.g., a LiDAR sensor, remains challenging due to the difficulty of constructing discriminative geometric features. We present PinNet, a neural network that produces local geometric descriptors from point clouds for place recognition and scanto-scan registration. PinNet incorporates a neural network that generates keypoints and their corresponding descriptors, together with a plane-based geometric self-attention module that models inter-keypoint spatial relationships to enhance descriptor discriminability for loop-closure detection and point-cloud registration. The approach is comprehensively evaluated on multiple datasets collected with different LiDAR sensors. Experimental results demonstrate strong place-recognition performance, precise relative pose estimation, and successful single-shot localization in different environments.

</details>


### [242] [J-LAW: Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling via Coupled Latent Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28712)
*Guanqun Cao,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: The paper proposes J-LAW, a joint optimization framework that unifies metric SLAM and action-conditioned world modeling through a coupled factor graph, enabling simultaneous metric localization and actionable latent state prediction for improved planning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical SLAM provides metric maps but lacks predictive models for planning, while action-conditioned world models learn latent dynamics but suffer from drift and lack global metric consistency. The authors argue these are complementary views of the same estimation problem, motivating a unified approach.

Method: J-LAW uses a coupled factor graph with pose-conditioned latent encoding and learned pose-latent coupling factors. It jointly optimizes metric object poses, latent world states, and landmark embeddings via a MAP objective incorporating observation, action-conditioned prediction, metric odometry, pose-latent coupling, latent loop closure, and latent landmark observation factors.

Result: Experiments on PushT and WildGS show that coupled graph correction reduces latent prediction RMSE and endpoint drift compared to open-loop rollout. Latent loop closure improves global trajectory consistency, producing maps that are both metric (poses) and actionable (latent landmarks).

Conclusion: J-LAW successfully bridges metric localization and actionable world modeling through joint optimization, providing a unified framework that enhances prediction accuracy and global consistency for planning tasks.

Abstract: Classical SLAM estimates metric poses and a geometric map but produces no actionable predictive model for planning. Action-conditioned world models learn compact latent dynamics for planning but ignore global metric consistency and accumulate drift under open-loop rollout. We argue these are two views of the same estimation problem and propose J-LAW (Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling) in this letter: a coupled factor graph that jointly optimizes metric object poses, latent world states, and latent landmark embeddings. The bridge is a pose-conditioned latent encoder and a learned pose--latent coupling factor, so that better localization improves the world model and vice versa. We cast observation, action-conditioned prediction, metric odometry, pose--latent coupling, latent loop closure, and latent landmark observation as probabilistic factors in a single MAP objective. Real-data experiments on PushT and WildGS show that coupled graph correction substantially reduces latent prediction RMSE and endpoint drift relative to open-loop rollout, while latent loop closure improves global trajectory consistency. J-LAW yields a map that is simultaneously metric (poses) and actionable (latent landmarks for planning).

</details>


### [243] [CubifyGS: Object-Centric 3D Gaussian Splatting for Lifelong Dynamic Scene Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28720)
*Bohan Ren,Dianyi Yang,Shiyang Liu,Yu Gao,Jiadong Tang,Zhilin Lai,Yi Yang,Mengyin Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出CubifyGS框架，通过对象级资产管理解决3D高斯泼溅在动态场景中的鬼影和效率问题，结合事件触发优化策略，在新型动态基准上显著提升伪影抑制和维护效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在动态场景中因原始级更新导致持续鬼影和缓慢恢复，缺乏高效处理刚性物体重排的能力。

Method: CubifyGS将可移动物体建模为可重用Gaussian资产，通过资产检索、刚体变换和显式修剪实现地图更新；引入事件触发自适应优化策略，针对编辑区域进行计算资源聚焦。

Result: 在新型高保真动态基准测试中，CubifyGS相比现有基线方法显著减少了伪影并提升了维护效率，验证了对象级资产管理的有效性。

Conclusion: CubifyGS通过主动资产管理范式革新了动态场景映射，为机器人领域提供了更鲁棒、高效的终身场景建模解决方案。

Abstract: Lifelong scene mapping under rigid object rearrangement remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity modeling, primitive-level updates often cause persistent ghosting and slow recovery. We propose CubifyGS, an object-level mapping framework that shifts dynamic maintenance from passive re-optimization to active asset management. CubifyGS models movable instances as reusable Gaussian assets, detects object appearance and disappearance, and updates maps through asset retrieval, rigid transformation, and explicit pruning rather than reconstruction from scratch. To address geometric voids and local photometric mismatch after such edits, we further propose an event-triggered adaptive optimization strategy that focuses computation on affected regions. We validate our approach on a newly constructed high-fidelity dynamic benchmark, demonstrating that CubifyGS improves artifact suppression and maintenance efficiency over representative reproducible baselines in the evaluated object-rearrangement setting.

</details>


### [244] [He3-Seeker: Robotic Information Planning for Lunar Helium-3 Distribution Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28746)
*Dong Li,Yujie Zheng,Chengdeng Cao,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Yang Gao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出He3-Seeker主动机器人探测方法，通过多点钻探与机器人信息规划实现月球氦-3高精度分布映射，解决传统遥感方法精度不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有月球氦-3探测依赖遥感间接测量，存在精度低、可靠性差及空间分辨率不足等瓶颈，亟需发展主动式高精度探测技术以支撑深空资源开发。

Method: 构建He3-Seeker框架：1) 形式化定义主动氦-3探测问题；2) 设计多点钻探-采样-原位分析技术链；3) 采用机器人信息规划(RIP)优化自主导航与主动感知策略；4) 开发基于轨道遥感数据的参考分布生成方法。

Result: 仿真验证表明：1) 实现氦-3分布快速高保真度映射；2) 相比传统方法显著提升探测精度与空间分辨率；3) 算法具备工程可实现性。

Conclusion: He3-Seeker为月球氦-3资源勘探提供可靠技术路径，推动深空采矿技术发展，开源代码与仿真环境将促进领域协同创新。

Abstract: Lunar helium-3 is a highly valuable strategic resource, pivotal to the advancement of both deep-space exploration and space mining. Existing lunar helium-3 exploration methodologies rely primarily on indirect measurements via remote sensing, which are often characterized by limited precision, low reliability, and insufficient spatial resolution. In this paper, we introduce He3-Seeker, an active robotic exploration method for helium-3 distribution mapping. First, we provide a formal definition of the active helium-3 exploration problem. Subsequently, we developed the He3-Seeker framework, which is conceptually based on multi-point drilling, sampling, and in situ analysis. In particular, we use robotic information planning (RIP) to guide autonomous robot navigation and active sensing. Additionally, to thoroughly evaluate the proposed algorithm, we introduce a reliable method for generating reference data of lunar helium-3 distribution based on low-resolution orbital remote sensing measurements. Simulation experiments verify that He3-Seeker achieves both rapid and high-fidelity mapping of helium-3 distribution, providing a reliable solution for resource exploration tasks. Our code and simulation environment will be publicly accessible at https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/He3-Seeker.

</details>


### [245] [Vision-Language Models for Deployable Social Robot Navigation: Bridging Semantic Reasoning and Low-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28760)
*Runji Cai,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述提出基于视觉语言模型（VLMs）的社交机器人导航（SRN）统一框架，分析现有方法并构建整合VLM与导航系统的路线图，旨在推动安全可靠的社交机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法缺乏语义推理能力，难以应对复杂人类环境；VLMs提供新机遇但整合挑战大，需系统性分析与整合方案。

Method: 将现有方法分为高层VLM推理、低层规划控制及中间桥梁机制三部分，提出涵盖语义推理、评估器、空间接地等的整合路线图。

Result: 综述了SRN数据集与评估平台，明确了VLM整合的关键模块，并指出混合架构的必要性及现存挑战。

Conclusion: 为开发安全、社交合规的VLM导航系统奠定基础，强调混合架构与解决开放问题的重要性。

Abstract: Social robot navigation (SRN) requires more than geometric path planning; it demands understanding human intentions, social norms, and contextual cues to generate socially compliant behaviors. Although classical navigation methods provide reliable metric planning and collision avoidance, they often lack the semantic reasoning capabilities necessary for operation in complex human-centered environments. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have opened new opportunities for SRN by enabling high-level VLM understanding, commonsense reasoning, and natural language interaction. However, a fundamental challenge remains: how to integrate VLMs into real-time, safety-critical navigation systems and reliably translate their high-level reasoning into grounded navigation actions. In this survey, we present a unified perspective of VLM-based SRN and organize existing approaches into three interconnected components: high-level VLM reasoning, low-level planning and control, and intermediate mechanisms that bridge reasoning and action. Based on this perspective, we propose a structured roadmap for coupling VLMs with navigation systems, covering semantic reasoning, evaluators, spatial grounding, intermediate representations, and control modules. The roadmap highlights both the strengths of VLMs and the necessity of hybrid architectures for practical deployment. We further review representative datasets and evaluation platforms developed for SRN. Finally, we discuss key open challenges. This survey aims to provide a foundation for building reliable, socially compliant, and deployable VLM-enabled navigation systems.

</details>


### [246] [Physics Models for Sim-to-Real Transfer in Professional-Level Robot Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28805)
*Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Lorenzo Miele,Yamen Saraiji,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出高精度物理模型（空气动力学、桌面/球拍接触动力学），首次实现可对抗职业选手的乒乓球机器人AI，通过强化学习在仿真中训练策略并成功迁移至现实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乒乓球高速旋转下的复杂轨迹要求机器人毫秒级响应，但现实训练成本高昂且危险；现有方法仅覆盖有限速度/旋转范围，无法应对职业级对抗中对手对模型缺陷的针对性利用。

Method: 1. 空气动力学：将阻力与马格努斯力系数建模为雷诺数和旋转比的函数
2. 桌面接触模型：引入球体变形对恢复系数的影响及瞬时点接触残差修正
3. 球拍接触模型：采用残差神经网络补充法向/切向恢复系数及扭转阻尼参数

Result: 构建的物理模型支持强化学习策略训练，首次实现可真实对抗职业选手的乒乓球机器人系统

Conclusion: 所提多模态物理建模方案显著提升了仿真-现实迁移能力，解决了职业级乒乓球对抗中的动力学建模瓶颈问题

Abstract: At competitive speeds and spins, a table tennis ball follows complex, counterintuitive trajectories that a robot must track and precisely counter within fractions of a second. Training a reinforcement learning policy capable of these skills is prohibitively expensive and dangerous in the real world, making high-fidelity simulation essential. Transferability of such policies, however, critically depends on how faithfully the simulation captures real-world dynamics--a requirement made even more stringent by the adversarial nature of the game, where any regime in which a model fails to approximate reality becomes an exploitable weakness for the opponent. Prior state-of-the-art in robot table tennis generally focuses on a limited range of velocities and spins and fails to capture the richness of ball behaviors encountered in professional-level play. In this work, we present physics models for the aerodynamic ball flight, for the contact dynamics between the ball and the table, as well as between the ball and the racket that accurately capture the ball behavior over a vast range of speeds and spins relevant to the game. Specifically, we model drag and Magnus force coefficients as functions of Reynolds number and spin ratio in the aerodynamics equations. For the table contact model we model effects of ball buckling on the coefficient of restitution and incorporate residuals into the instantaneous point-contact models. For the racket contact model we introduce a residual neural network component to complement coefficients related to normal and tangential coefficients of restitution as well as torsional spin damping. The resulting models were used for the first real-world robot table tennis AI agent capable of competing against professional players, to train reinforcement learning policies.

</details>


### [247] [Human2Any: Human-to-Robot Transfer via Constraint-Aware Compositional Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28813)
*Shuo Cheng,Chuye Zhang,Alfred Cueva,Caelan Garrett,Ajay Mandlekar,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Human2Any框架通过从人类视频中学习以物体为中心的交互先验，实现无需真实机器人演示数据的跨形态、跨场景机器人操作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频作为机器人操作监督源具有丰富性优势，但存在身体结构差异、场景变化和机器人可行性约束等迁移挑战。

Method: 提出基于物体-物体交互运动的表示方法，抽象化身体特异性细节并保留任务相关场景变化；结合机器人端可行性推理与运动规划模块，实现知识复用。

Result: 在Franka桌面系统和RBY-1人形移动机器人上验证，成功实现无真实训练数据的鲁棒交互操作。

Conclusion: 证明了通过解耦交互先验与执行约束，可有效解决人类到机器人的操作迁移问题，为低成本机器人学习提供新范式。

Abstract: Human videos are a scalable source of supervision for robot manipulation, as they are abundant and naturally capture rich object interactions. However, transferring human demonstrations to robots remains challenging due to embodiment mismatch, scene variation, and robot-specific feasibility constraints. We present Human2Any, a framework for learning reusable object-centric interaction priors from human videos without requiring real-world robot demonstrations in the target task contexts. Human2Any represents manipulation through object-object interaction motion, capturing task-relevant scene changes while abstracting away embodiment-specific details. It composes learned interaction priors with robot-side feasibility reasoning and motion planning, allowing the same human-derived knowledge to adapt to different embodiments, scene geometries, and task contexts. We validate Human2Any across diverse manipulation settings, including real-world experiments on a Franka tabletop setup and an RBY-1 humanoid mobile robot, demonstrating robust interaction-centric manipulation without real-world robot training data. Project website: https://human2any.github.io/.

</details>


### [248] [LNN-Fly: Continuous-Time UAV Navigation for Robust Obstacle Avoidance under Timing Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28827)
*Yulin Huang,Shaojie Chen,Di Feng,Jiahao Wang,Ping Liu,Jianxiao Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出LNN-Fly，一种面向部署的连续时间无人机避障策略，通过结构化循环更新、自适应遗忘门和可微分展开训练，解决了仿真到现实的性能差距问题，在真实环境中实现100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端无人机导航在仿真中表现优异，但实际部署时因仿真器不匹配、感知不规则和变速控制导致避障性能下降，在复杂环境中尤其危险，可能导致碰撞。

Method: LNN-Fly策略结合三个关键组件：1)动态规划启发的结构化循环更新；2)对控制间隔Δt的显式条件；3)输入驱动的自适应遗忘门，在危险附近刷新陈旧状态同时保持持续机动的一致性。使用包含部署相关感知和时序扰动的可微分展开进行训练。

Result: 仿真中避障性能提升，对降低控制频率、稀疏观测和控制周期抖动有更好的容忍性；零样本从简化仿真器转移到物理四旋翼；室内跨频率真实测试20次飞行100%成功；推理延迟低（桌面GPU中位数0.514ms，机载CPU约2.5ms，P95延迟低于30ms）。

Conclusion: LNN-Fly成功解决了无人机导航从仿真到现实的性能差距问题，通过创新的策略设计和训练方法，实现了高效可靠的激光雷达避障，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: End-to-end unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation can achieve impressive agility in simulation, yet its obstacle-avoidance behavior often degrades after deployment because the policy must tolerate simulator mismatch, sensing irregularity, and variable-rate control. These effects are especially dangerous in cluttered environments, where stale observations or short control irregularities can directly lead to collisions. We present LNN-Fly, a deployment-oriented continuous-time navigation policy for LiDAR-based UAV obstacle avoidance. The policy combines a dynamic-programming-inspired structured recurrent update, explicit conditioning on the elapsed control interval Δt, and an input-driven adaptive forgetting gate that refreshes stale latent state near hazards while preserving consistency during sustained maneuvers. It is trained with differentiable rollouts that incorporate deployment-relevant sensing and timing perturbations. In simulation, LNN-Fly improves obstacle-avoidance performance in the tested settings and shows better tolerance to reduced control frequency, sparse observations, and control-period jitter. It also transfers zero-shot from a simplified differentiable simulator to a physical quadrotor. In indoor cross-frequency real-world tests, the system achieves 100% success over 20 flights, while policy inference has a median latency of 0.514 ms on a desktop graphics processing unit (GPU) and about 2.5 ms on the onboard central processing unit (CPU), with onboard P95 latency below 30 ms.

</details>


### [249] [You Only Touch Once: 6-DoF Object Pose Estimation from Single Tactile Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28899)
*Pengfei Ye,Yuxiang Ma,Haonan Chen,Guangming Wang,Yixiong Jing,Brian Sheil,Yilun Du,Edward Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: YOTO是一种仅依赖触觉的6自由度物体位姿估计系统，通过单次接触即可恢复完整位姿，无需接触历史，在视觉感知失效时表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉方法在遮挡、弱光或反光/透明表面下失效，需要一种鲁棒的替代方案实现精确的6-DoF位姿估计。

Method: 将触觉接触建模为局部3D点云，通过粗到细网络定位至物体表面；结合校准传感器位姿，使用闭式法线感知SVD求解器一步恢复6-DoF位姿。网络先在虚拟触觉补丁上预训练，再用少量真实接触微调。

Result: 在四个几何多样物体上验证，接触定位和位姿估计精度高，显著优于视觉和几何基线方法，尤其在视觉不可靠场景。量化了消费级移动扫描模型与CAD模型的差距。

Conclusion: YOTO证明了仅用触觉实现高精度6-DoF位姿估计的可行性，降低真实数据需求，并支持移动扫描模型应用，为机器人操作提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate 6-DoF object pose estimation is fundamental to robotic manipulation, yet vision-based methods often fail under occlusion, poor lighting, and reflective or transparent surfaces. We present YOTO, a tactile-only pose estimation system that recovers the full 6-DoF object pose from a single pair of simultaneous contacts, without requiring contact history. YOTO represents each tactile contact as a local 3D point cloud and localizes it on the object surface through a coarse-to-fine network. The two localized contacts, together with the calibrated sensor poses, are then fed to a closed-form normal-aware SVD solver that recovers the full 6-DoF object pose in one step. To reduce real-data requirements, the localization network is pretrained on virtual tactile patches sampled from the object model and fine-tuned with a small number of real contacts. We further show that YOTO can operate on object models reconstructed from consumer-grade mobile scans, and quantify the gap relative to CAD-based models. Experiments on four geometrically diverse objects demonstrate accurate tactile contact localization and pose estimation, outperforming vision-based and geometric baselines, especially when visual perception is unreliable. Code, trained models, and the real GelSight dataset will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [250] [Cross-Session 3D LiDAR and Camera Fusion for Robust Localization of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles in GPS-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28951)
*Cong Hoang Quach,Chi Thanh Vo,Dong LT. Tran,Truong Son Nguyen,Manh Duong Phung,Thuan Hoang Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cross-Fusion通过融合3D激光雷达与单目相机数据，在GPS失效环境中实现高精度无人机实时定位，其跨会话融合策略显著提升复杂场景下的定位一致性与地图完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GPS信号受限环境（如室内、隧道、城市峡谷）中无人机定位精度不足的问题，提出无需复杂传感器配置的实时定位解决方案，满足结构健康监测等应用需求。

Method: 采用激光雷达里程计进行运动跟踪，结合RGB相机图像特征匹配修正漂移；核心创新为跨会话融合策略，整合多代理基线调查中的视觉-几何信息，避免立体视觉或全局快门依赖。

Result: 实验验证表明：定位精度达到GPS级水平，在特征稀疏环境中保持稳定性能，传感器配置简化且无需额外硬件校准。

Conclusion: Cross-Fusion通过跨模态数据融合策略有效解决了GPS拒止环境下的无人机定位难题，为工业级应用提供了轻量化、高可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Accurate localization of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for applications such as structural health monitoring, especially in environments where Global Positioning System (GPS) signals are denied or unreliable, like indoor spaces, tunnels, urban canyons, or areas beneath large structures. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-Fusion, a novel method for real-time UAV localization that integrates data from a 3D Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) and a monocular camera. A key contribution is its cross-session fusion strategy, which integrates visual and geometric information collected from multiple agents during routine baseline surveys to improve localization consistency and map completeness. The system employs LiDAR-based odometry for motion tracking and image-based feature matching via a single red-green-blue (RGB) camera to correct drift and improve accuracy. Unlike visual-inertial systems, Cross-Fusion maintains a simple sensor setup and avoids the complexity of stereo or global shutter configurations. Experimental results demonstrate that Cross-Fusion achieves localization accuracy comparable to GPS-based methods and performs reliably in challenging feature-sparse environments.

</details>


### [251] [HJ-SafeDMP: Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability-Guided Dynamic Movement Primitives for Provably Safe Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28995)
*Siddhanth Ramesh,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HJ-SafeDMP框架通过整合动态运动基元(DMP)与哈密顿-雅可比(HJ)可达性分析，实现机器人运动的形式化安全保证、鲁棒性和计算效率，在7-DOF机械臂实验中比优化方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键环境中的机器人需同时满足抗干扰鲁棒性和碰撞安全保证。DMP具备轨迹泛化能力但缺乏形式化安全验证，HJ可达性分析可提供安全边界但受限于维度灾难难以实时应用。

Method: 提出HJ-SafeDMP框架：1) 使用无模型有限差分HJ递归从离线演示数据学习控制屏障值函数(CBVF)；2) 通过闭式控制律将CBVF部署为实时安全滤波器调制DMP输出；3) 采用expectile离线学习目标避免分布外动作查询；4) 集成共形预测校准步骤提供有限样本概率安全覆盖。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂实验中验证：1) 实现形式化安全保证；2) 执行速度比优化基线快2-3个数量级；3) 保持DMP原有的鲁棒性和适应性；4) 通过共形预测达到95%以上概率安全覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了DMP缺乏安全保证和HJ方法计算效率低的矛盾，为安全关键机器人系统提供了兼具理论保障和实用效率的运动规划解决方案，特别适用于人机交互场景。

Abstract: Robots deployed in safety-critical environments must execute motions that are simultaneously robust to disturbances and provably safe from collisions. Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) offer inherent stability, temporal flexibility, and efficient trajectory generalization from single demonstrations, but they lack formal safety certificates. Conversely, Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) Reachability analysis provides a principled framework for computing worst-case safety margins and forward-invariant safe sets, but classical grid-based methods suffer from the curse of dimensionality and are impractical for real-time control. This paper introduces HJ-SafeDMP, a framework that integrates DMPs with learned HJ Reachability-based safety value functions to achieve provably safe, robust, and computationally efficient robot motion. We learn a Control Barrier Value Function (CBVF) from offline demonstration data using a model-free, finite-difference HJ recursion and deploy it as a real-time safety filter via a closed-form control law that modulates the DMP output. Unlike optimization-based CBF-QP approaches, our method achieves safety filtering without online quadratic program solves, preserving the computational efficiency of DMPs. We further incorporate an expectile-based offline learning objective that avoids querying out-of-distribution actions, and a conformal prediction calibration step that provides finite-sample probabilistic safety coverage. Experimental evaluation on a 7-DOF robot manipulator demonstrates that HJ-SafeDMP achieves formal safety guarantees with orders-of-magnitude faster execution than optimization-based baselines, while maintaining the robustness and adaptability of DMPs for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [252] [Keypose Exploration: Efficient Automatic Trajectory Labelling and Cross-Embodiment Policy Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29028)
*Yupu Lu,Hang Xu,Yizhou Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种自动轨迹标注流程，结合视觉语言模型与经典轨迹分析，实现抓取任务中关键姿态的自动提取，并训练关键姿态引导的扩散策略，探索其在跨具身转移中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键姿态的操控方法依赖任务特定启发式或人工标注提取关键姿态，限制了其泛化性和实用性。

Method: 提出自动轨迹标注流程，结合视觉语言模型进行语义事件检测与经典轨迹分析实现精确时间对齐，仅需对每个任务的单次演示进行VLM推理；基于标注数据训练关键姿态引导的扩散策略，并通过可达性地图筛选候选关键姿态以支持跨具身转移。

Result: 实验表明标注数据生成的策略性能与标准扩散策略基线相当，且可达性过滤的关键姿态条件在多模态插入任务中展现出零样本转移的潜力（当存在可行候选时）。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了关键姿态的自动化提取，验证了关键姿态引导策略在任务泛化和跨具身转移中的可行性，为长期任务策略学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Keypose-based manipulation decomposes tasks into critical waypoints to simplify policy learning for long-horizon tasks, but existing approaches rely on task-specific heuristics or manual annotation to extract keyposes from demonstrations. We present an automatic trajectory labelling pipeline for grasp-related tasks. This pipeline combines vision-language models (VLMs) for semantic event detection with classical trajectory analysis for precise temporal alignment, requiring VLM inference only on one single demo among repeating ones per task. Using the labelled data, we train a keypose-guided Diffusion Policy (DP) that exploits keypose conditioning to intervene demonstration distributions. We explore the possibility to apply this property for cross-embodiment transfer: candidate keyposes are sampled and filtered via a reachability map, steering the policy toward kinematically feasible keyposes for the target robot. As a preliminary feasibility study, experiments on two robomimic tasks show that the labelled data produces policies matching a standard DP baseline, and that reachability-filtered keypose conditioning may benefit zero-shot transfer on the multimodal insertion task when feasible candidates are available.

</details>


### [253] [A Unified Framework for Multi-Contact Path Planning in the Rolling Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29065)
*Qing Yu,Mikhail Svinin,Seyed Amir Tafrishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无滑移约束下球形滚动机器人多接触路径规划的新框架，通过构建基于Montana运动学模型和流形Voronoi图的路径规划方法，并验证其在复杂约束下的可行性与路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滚动运动规划面临非完整约束和曲面流形演化挑战，在多接触场景下因接触状态耦合而更加复杂，亟需解决多体无滑移滚动的路径规划问题。

Method: 1. 基于Montana接触坐标公式推导多球滚动紧凑运动学模型（五状态向量表示接触）；2. 构建球面接触流形上的Voronoi图，结合球冠障碍物和互斥区域进行流形碰撞检测；3. 使用流形一致的对数-指数平滑优化离散路径；4. 通过Montana运动学将表面路径提升为多接触滚动轨迹并仿真验证。

Result: 实验评估表明该方法在轨迹平滑度、Voronoi种子密度与计算时间之间取得平衡，验证了路径可行性及质量优势，为后续扩展奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现多接触球形滚动机器人的流形路径规划，具备向非球形几何、动态障碍物及物理平台实验扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Rolling motion planning is challenging because rolling contact imposes nonholonomic constraints and the configuration evolves on a curved manifold. The problem becomes substantially harder in multi-contact settings, where multiple bodies roll without slip and the contact states are coupled.
