Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的视觉导航规划器采用验证中心范式，存在候选依赖、计算开销大及动作与视觉预测不一致的问题，亟需更高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: SWAM采用任务导向的联合生成框架，训练时使用深度伪标签内化空间先验，推理时仅需单目RGB输入。通过视觉引导动作优化模块和轨迹尺度正则化损失，实现动作与视觉线索的细粒度对齐及多尺度预测稳定性。

Result: 实验表明SWAM在成功率、轨迹准确性和推理效率上显著优于现有两阶段方法，且在未见环境中展现出鲁棒的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SWAM通过端到端联合生成机制有效解决了传统方法的局限性，为视觉导航提供了更高效、准确的解决方案，同时验证了其在复杂环境中的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Existing world model-based planners for visual navigation typically follow a verification-centric paradigm, decoupling goal intent from trajectory synthesis. This approach suffers from candidate dependence, heavy computational overhead, and inconsistencies between sampled actions and predicted visuals. To address these issues, we propose SWAM (Spatial-perceiving World Action Model), a task-centric joint observation-action generation framework. Given start and goal RGB observations, SWAM performs single-pass inference to simultaneously generate intermediate RGB-D sequences and corresponding action trajectories, promoting goal-consistent trajectory generation and improved spatial feasibility. While SWAM leverages depth pseudo-labels during training to internalize spatial priors, it requires only monocular RGB input at inference time. We further introduce a visual-guided action refinement module and a trajectory-scale regularization loss to enforce fine-grained alignment between motion and visual cues while stabilizing predictions across varying distances. Extensive experiments show that SWAM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art two-stage planners in success rate, trajectory accuracy, and inference efficiency, while demonstrating robust zero-shot generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


### [291] [Sphere-VIO: Fast and Robust Visual-Inertial Odometry via Unified Spherical Representation for Heterogeneous Multi-Camera Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29910)
*Yueteng Yang,Yusen Xie,Hao Wei,Qianhao Wang,Boyu Zhou,Fei Gao,Jun Ma,Jinni Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sphere-VIO提出了一种基于统一球面表示的轻量级多摄像头VIO框架，解决了异构多摄像头系统兼容性问题，通过球面全景模型、分层特征对齐和ESKF后端优化，在准确性、鲁棒性和实时性间实现更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIO算法多针对固定摄像头配置设计，缺乏对异构多摄像头系统的统一支持；同时因缺少跨摄像头统一表示和关联机制，难以兼顾鲁棒特征跟踪、稳定深度估计与实时性能。

Method: 1) 提出统一球面全景模型(USPM)，支持标准相机模型并实现多摄像头图像与共享球面空间的双向快速映射；2) 设计分层全向特征对齐(HOFA)管道，结合全局球面约束实现跨摄像头匹配，并融合深度观测初始化；3) 开发多摄像头适配的ESKF后端，采用球面方位残差和舒尔补边缘化降低计算开销。

Result: 在公开基准和自定义全向数据集上的实验表明，Sphere-VIO在准确性、鲁棒性、效率和跨摄像头通用性方面取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过统一球面表示和高效算法设计，有效解决了异构多摄像头VIO系统的兼容性和性能瓶颈，为资源受限设备上的实时状态估计提供了新方案。

Abstract: Multi-camera visual-inertial odometry (VIO) overcomes the inherent limitations of pure visual systems by expanding the field of view. However, existing algorithms are typically tailored for fixed camera setups and lack unified compatibility with heterogeneous multi-camera systems. Meanwhile, due to the absence of a unified cross-camera representation and association mechanism, current methods struggle to achieve a balance among robust cross-camera feature tracking, stable depth estimation, and reliable real-time performance. To address these issues, we present Sphere-VIO, a lightweight filter-based VIO framework with unified spherical representation for heterogeneous multi-camera systems. Specifically, we first propose a Unified Spherical Panorama Model (USPM) that supports all standard camera models and enables bidirectional fast mapping between multi-camera images and a shared spherical space without sequential stitching, simplifying cross-camera feature management and improving triangulation efficiency. Second, we design a parallel-accelerated depth-guided semi-direct tracking pipeline, namely Hierarchical Omnidirectional Feature Alignment (HOFA), with global spherical constraints for robust cross-camera matching, and fuse multi-camera depth observations into a standard depth filter for stable initialization. Finally, we develop a multi-camera-adapted ESKF backend that employs spherical bearing residuals and Schur complement marginalization to minimize computational overhead, enabling accurate real-time state estimation on resource-constrained devices. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and a custom omnidirectional dataset show that Sphere-VIO achieves superior trade-offs between accuracy, robustness, efficiency, and cross-camera generality.

</details>


### [292] [Flying to Image-Specified Objects: 3D Quadrotor Navigation via Cross-Graph Memory and Viewpoint Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29917)
*Junjie Gao,Yuqi Chen,Yongzhou Pan,Yaosheng Deng,Jiaping Xiao,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种分层无人机导航框架，通过视角感知动作节点生成、轻量级语义记忆和基于学习的策略选择，解决实例特定图像目标导航中的3D控制与安全挑战，仿真与实际验证均显示性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机实例图像导航面临连续3D控制、有限视野及安全约束的挑战，传统方法难以有效选择信息丰富的视角以定位目标实例。

Method: 设计分层框架分离高层决策与低层执行：1) 生成前沿区域/目标对象周围的视角感知动作节点；2) 轻量级语义记忆维护对象与观察上下文；3) 学习策略选择最优动作节点；4) 轨迹规划器生成安全可行的3D路径。

Result: 仿真实验显示相比基线方法有显著提升，实际飞行验证了框架的实用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过分层决策与语义记忆机制有效解决了无人机实例导航难题，为复杂环境下的自主导航提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Instance-Specific Image-Goal Navigation (InstanceImageNav) requires a robot to navigate toward the exact object instance depicted in a query image. Extending this task to quadrotors is challenging due to continuous 3D control, limited field of view (FOV), and safety constraints, which make successful navigation highly dependent on selecting informative viewpoints. We propose a hierarchical navigation framework for quadrotor InstanceImageNav that separates high-level decision making from low-level motion execution. Instead of navigating directly to spatial locations, the system generates viewpoint-aware action nodes around frontier regions and potential target objects, enabling the robot to explore while maintaining informative viewpoints for detecting the target instance. A lightweight semantic memory maintains object-level and observation-level context, allowing semantic cues to propagate to candidate action nodes for decision making. A learning-based policy selects the most promising action node, and a trajectory planner generates dynamically feasible 3D flight paths for safe execution. Experiments in simulation demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, and real-world quadrotor flights validate the practicality and robustness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [293] [RoamFlow: Reinforcement-Aligned One-Step Action MeanFlow Policy for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29934)
*Zixuan Zhang,Yuqi Chen,Junjie Gao,Siyuan Song,Yongzhou Pan,Beichen Wang,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoamFlow是一个生成式导航框架，通过MeanFlow预测平均速度场和两阶段训练策略，在模拟与真实环境中实现高效、低延迟的图像目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法直接映射感知到动作，难以建模长期依赖，导致次优轨迹和推理效率低下。

Method: 提出RoamFlow框架，利用MeanFlow预测平均速度场进行轨迹生成，并结合专家模仿初始化与强化学习策略优化的两阶段训练方法。

Result: 在Habitat仿真和真实机器人平台实验中，RoamFlow在保持强导航性能的同时显著降低推理延迟，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: RoamFlow通过生成式建模与混合训练策略有效解决了长期依赖问题，为具身机器人导航提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation is a key challenge in embodied robotics, where an agent must reach a target specified solely by a goal image. While existing reinforcement learning approaches map perceptual observations directly to actions, they struggle to model long-horizon dependencies, often leading to suboptimal trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose RoamFlow, a generative navigation framework that leverages MeanFlow to predict the average velocity field for trajectory synthesis, enabling efficient few-step generation and reducing inference latency. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy that combines expert imitation for stable initialization with reinforcement learning for task-specific policy refinement. Extensive experiments in both Habitat simulation and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that RoamFlow achieves efficient inference while maintaining strong navigation performance under real-time constraints.

</details>


### [294] [OpenSPM: An Environment-Transferable Robotic Key Spatial Pose Memory and Closed-Loop High-Frequency Flow-Matching Action Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29936)
*Iok Tong Lei,Qingchen Xie,Yifan Wang,Yap Ying Jie,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSPM框架通过空间持久记忆和流匹配模型实现开放环境机器人操作，在LIBERO-GOAL任务上达到85.6%成功率和1033.3 Hz控制频率，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境桌面机器人操作需兼顾语义理解、几何姿态估计和高频动作生成，但现有端到端视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型缺乏显式几何约束且训练成本高，难以弥合高层语义与底层物理执行间的差距。

Method: 提出OpenSPM框架：利用语义条件3D感知和卡尔曼滤波跟踪6D姿态；从人类演示提取关键空间姿态作为对象中心持久记忆；推理时基于自然语言指令检索记忆、通过SE(3)变换迁移至新场景，并用轻量级条件流匹配模型生成高频动作块；结合实时本体感知反馈和终端残差校正抑制误差累积。

Result: 在十个LIBERO-GOAL任务中，成功率达85.6%，等效控制频率1033.3 Hz，推理计算力需求最小；消融实验验证结构化空间持久记忆和闭环残差校正的关键作用。

Conclusion: OpenSPM有效解决了开放环境机器人操作中的几何约束和计算效率问题，结构化空间持久记忆与闭环校正机制是实现可靠高频操作的核心。

Abstract: Open-environment tabletop robotic manipulation requires systems to possess semantic understanding, precise geometric pose estimation, and high-frequency action generation. While end-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) models excel at semantic generalization, they often lack explicit geometric constraints for fine-grained tasks and require costly training. To bridge the gap between high-level semantics and low-level physical execution, we propose OpenSPM, an open environment spatial persistent memory framework consisting of spatial pose memory and flow-matching action generation model. OpenSPM first leverages semantically conditioned 3D perception and Kalman filtering to track continuous 6D poses. It then extracts key spatial poses from human demonstrations, keeping them as transferable, object-centric spatial persistent memory entries. During inference, OpenSPM retrieves relevant memory entries in terms of natural language instructions, transfers the spatial poses to new scenes using SE(3) transformations, and generates high-frequency action chunks via a lightweight conditional flow-matching model. Combined with real-time proprioceptive state feedback and terminal residual correction, the system effectively suppresses trajectory error accumulation. Evaluated on ten LIBERO-GOAL tasks, OpenSPM achieves an 85.6% success rate and an equivalent control frequency of 1033.3 Hz, while requiring minimal inference AI computing power. Extensive ablations illustrate that structured spatial persistent memory and closed-loop residual correction play a crucial role in reliable, high-frequency robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [295] [REPAIR-Bench: A Benchmark for Robot Error Perception And Interaction Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29937)
*Giuliano Pioldi,Yashika Batra,Arman Ibrayeva,Yuanchen Bai,Purnjay Maruur,Promise Ekpo,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出REPAIR-Bench基准测试，通过214次人机交互实验（41名参与者）构建多模态数据集，涵盖四种机器人故障类型及同步生理/行为数据。该基准突破传统独立事件假设和二元检测局限，提出三阶段评估任务：跨会话故障检测、细粒度故障分类及用户偏好恢复策略预测。实验验证层次循环模型在故障检测（F1=0.80）和QLoRA微调大模型在恢复预测（Hit@5=0.76）的有效性，为可信人机交互系统提供标准化评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在三大局限：(1) 将故障视为孤立事件，忽略用户适应性变化；(2) 仅关注二元故障检测，缺乏细粒度分类；(3) 依赖规则化恢复策略，未考虑用户偏好。

Method: 构建REPAIR-Bench数据集（214次交互/41参与者），包含四种故障类型的多模态数据（面部动作单元、头部姿态、语音转录、情感报告）。设计三阶段评估任务：①跨会话故障检测建模用户适应过程；②视觉故障类型细粒度分类；③基于交互上下文预测用户偏好恢复策略。采用层次循环模型和大语言模型微调进行基准测试。

Result: 层次循环模型在故障检测任务中严格F1值达0.80（较单会话模型提升12.9%），故障定位平均符号误差-0.51秒，中位绝对误差2.97秒。QLoRA微调Mistral-7B在恢复预测任务中Hit@5=0.76，F1@5=0.32。

Conclusion: REPAIR-Bench为HRI和医疗机器人领域提供标准化故障评估框架，推动构建透明、自适应的可信恢复系统，突破传统规则化方法局限，支持用户中心化的故障处理范式。

Abstract: Understanding how users perceive and respond to robot failures is essential for building robust and trustworthy robot systems. Prior work, however, (i) often treats failures as independent events, (ii) emphasizes binary failure detection, (iii) with rule-based recovery modeling. We present REPAIR-Bench, built on 214 interaction trials from 41 participants, the benchmark spans four induced failure types and provides synchronized facial action units, head pose, speech transcripts, and post-interaction affect and recovery reports. The benchmark spans three novel evaluation tasks that jointly capture the lifecycle of failure in human-robot interaction (HRI): (i) failure detection over inter-dependent interaction sessions, modeling longitudinal user adaptation across repeated failures; (ii) visual failure-type classification beyond binary success/failure formulations; and (iii) user-centered recovery prediction, inferring users' preferred recovery strategies from interaction context rather than relying on manually designed or rule-based strategies. In baseline experiments, hierarchical recurrent modeling improved failure detection over a single-session model (strict F1: 0.80 vs. 0.68), achieved a failure localization mean signed error of -0.51 s, median absolute error of 2.97 s and, for recovery prediction, a QLoRA-tuned Mistral-7B reached Hit@5=0.76 and F1@5=0.32. REPAIR-Bench provides both the HRI and Medical HRI communities with a standardized framework for (1) evaluating robot failures and (2) building transparent, adaptive, and trustworthy recovery systems.

</details>


### [296] [WARP: Whole-Body Retargeting for Learning from Offline Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29940)
*Zhenyang Chen,Chuizheng Kong,Chuye Zhang,Yuanshao Yang,Lawrence Y. Zhu,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WARP是一个离线重定向框架，通过显式建模具身差异，首次实现从人类演示到机器人全身移动操作的零样本迁移，无需遥操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据虽具扩展性，但存在显著具身差异；现有重定向方法因动作多模态导致策略收敛不可靠，亟需精确一致的动作提取方案。

Method: 采用闭式肩肘腕几何求解器实现精确末端执行器跟踪，结合惰性移动基座控制，在保留全身结构意图的同时生成唯一机器人轨迹。

Result: 实验验证WARP能生成高可靠性开环重放数据，实现零样本全身移动操作，消除对人类遥操作数据的依赖。

Conclusion: WARP开创了离线人类演示直接驱动机器人全身操作的新范式，为可扩展移动操作提供关键技术突破。

Abstract: Direct transfer from human demonstration to learnable robot action is a crucial step towards scalable whole-body mobile manipulation. While human data scales better than mobile teleoperation, it requires overcoming significant embodiment gaps. Existing retargeting methods yield imprecise or inconsistent solutions, causing action multi-modality that prevents supervised policies from reliably converging. We present Whole-body-Aware Retargeting from human Pose (WARP), an offline pipeline that explicitly models embodiment differences to extract precise, unique whole-body actions. WARP leverages a closed-form Shoulder-Elbow-Wrist (SEW) geometric solver for exact end-effector tracking while preserving whole-body structural intent. Paired with lazy mobile-base control, it extracts accurate, consistent robot trajectories. Evaluations show WARP provides highly reliable data for open-loop real-world replay. To our knowledge, WARP is the first framework to achieve zero-shot whole-body mobile manipulation directly from offline human demonstrations, eliminating the need for human-in-the-loop teleoperation action data. More details on https://warp-retarget.github.io/

</details>


### [297] [Seeing Touch from Motion: A Unified Modality-Aware Visuo-Tactile Policy with Tactile Motion Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29941)
*Shengqi Xu,Guojin Zhong,Yang Liu,Fanjie Wang,Hu Luo,Hanyu Zhou,Weiyao Zhang,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运动感知的触觉表示方法和基于混合Transformer的统一模态感知视觉-触觉策略，解决接触丰富操作中的精细接触状态区分和多模态融合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学触觉传感器在接触丰富操作中面临两大挑战：1) 原始图像或累积运动场表示存在感知模糊，难以区分细微接触变化；2) 视觉-触觉多模态融合方法难以同时建模跨模态交互与保持模态特性。

Method: 1) 提出运动感知触觉表示，利用瞬态与累积运动的相关性显式区分精细接触状态；2) 设计混合Transformer架构的统一模态感知策略，实现跨模态互补性建模与模态特性保持。

Result: 该方法有效解决了触觉感知模糊问题，并通过统一架构实现了视觉-触觉模态的高效融合，在接触丰富操作任务中表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究通过挖掘触觉运动动态先验和优化多模态融合机制，为接触丰富操作提供了新的技术路径，显著提升了机器人对复杂接触场景的理解能力。

Abstract: Visuo-Tactile policies leveraging optical tactile sensors have shown great promise in contact-rich manipulation. These sensors achieve high spatial resolution and multi-dimensional force sensing by utilizing an internal camera to monitor the deformation of their elastic gel surface, thereby indirectly inferring tactile cues. Despite their advantages, extracting fine-grained contact states necessary for contact-rich manipulation remains an open challenge. Existing methods typically use either raw images or cumulative motion fields to represent tactile cues. However, both are prone to perception ambiguity. Raw tactile images mainly capture appearance changes, while cumulative motion fields only reflect the aggregate gel deformation. Consequently, distinct fine-grained contact states can exhibit highly similar patterns, making it difficult to explicitly distinguish subtle contact variations. To address this issue, we explore the dynamic priors of tactile motion and discover that the correlation between transient and cumulative motion can explicitly distinguish fine-grained contact states. Based on this insight, we propose a motion-aware tactile representation to facilitate contact-rich manipulation. Beyond tactile representation, effective fusion of tactile and visual modalities is also critical. Most existing fusion methods either directly concatenate features from each modality or train modality-specific networks separately and fuse their outputs. However, these strategies struggle to simultaneously model cross-modal interactions and preserve modality-specific characteristics. In this work, we take advantage of the Mixture-of-Transformers architecture and propose a unified modality-aware visuo-tactile policy that captures cross-modal complementarity while maintaining modality-specific properties.

</details>


### [298] [Heterogeneous Tactile Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29948)
*Jianxin Bi,Qiang Wang,Jayaram Reddy,Kelvin Lin,Soibkhon Khajikhanov,Ruihan Gao,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出异构触觉Transformer(HTT)框架，通过传感器特定编码器和共享Transformer主干，结合每模态掩码重建和跨模态对齐预训练，实现异构触觉传感器间的共享表示学习，并在触觉感知和操作任务中验证了其可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器具有固有的异构性，一个传感器训练的模型无法直接应用于其他传感器，这限制了从多样化触觉数据中学习接触丰富的操作策略的能力。需要解决异构传感器间共享表示学习的挑战。

Method: 提出异构触觉Transformer(HTT)框架，包含传感器特定的编码器和共享的Transformer主干。采用每模态掩码重建与配对传感器间跨模态对齐的预训练策略。使用新提出的异构配对触觉(HPT)数据集进行预训练，该数据集包含160万帧跨四种视觉和阵列式触觉传感器的同步配对帧。

Result: 在多个不同的触觉感知和真实世界操作任务中，HTT被证明能够学习可转移的表示，能够适应新任务以及以前未见过的传感器。

Conclusion: HTT框架成功解决了异构触觉传感器间的共享表示学习问题，为大规模触觉数据学习和接触丰富的操作策略提供了有效的解决方案，具有良好的可迁移性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are inherently heterogeneous: a model trained on one sensor cannot be directly used on another, which limits learning contact-rich manipulation policies from diverse tactile data at scale. To bridge this gap, we propose the Heterogeneous Tactile Transformer (HTT), a framework that learns shared tactile representations across heterogeneous sensors. HTT consists of sensor-specific encoders and a shared transformer trunk, and is pretrained with per-modality masked reconstruction together with cross-modal alignment between paired sensors. Pretraining uses our novel Heterogeneous Paired Tactile (HPT) dataset, containing 1.6M synchronized paired frames across four vision- and array-based tactile sensors. Across distinct tactile perception and real-world manipulation tasks, HTT is shown to learn transferable representations that adapt to new tasks and previously unseen sensors. Dataset, code, and model checkpoints will be released upon publication at https://jxbi1010.github.io/htt-gh-page/.

</details>


### [299] [SIR: Structured Image Representations for Explainable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30101)
*Paul Mattes,Jan Schwab,Jens Bosch,Nils Blank,Maximilian Xiling Li,Minh-Trung Tang,Moritz Haberland,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: The paper proposes Structured Image Representations (SIR) using scene graphs for robot policy learning, improving performance and interpretability over image-based methods by creating task-relevant sparse graphs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing robot policies based on visual embeddings lack explicit structure and are sensitive to visual distractions, leading to opaque decision-making processes that are difficult to interpret.

Method: SIR constructs a fully connected graph from image features, then learns to sparsify it end-to-end into a task-relevant sub-graph passed to the action generation model, enabling intrinsic explainability.

Result: On RoboCasa, sparse graph policies achieved 19.5% success rate vs. 14.81% for image-based baselines. Analysis of sub-graph deviations revealed dataset biases like spurious correlations and positional biases.

Conclusion: SIR enhances both performance and model interpretability, providing a powerful tool for analyzing robot decision-making and uncovering dataset biases through structured scene graph representations.

Abstract: Existing robot policies based on learned visual embeddings lack explicit structure and are sensitive to visual distractions. Thus, the representations that drive their behaviour are often opaque, making their decision-making process difficult to interpret. To address this, we introduce Structured Image Representations (SIR), a method that leverages Scene Graphs (SGs) as an intermediate representation for robot policy learning. Our approach first constructs a fully connected graph, using image-derived features as initial node representations. Then, a module learns to sparsify this graph end-to-end, creating a task-relevant sub-graph that is passed to the action generation model. This process makes our model intrinsically explainable. Evaluations on RoboCasa show that our sparse graph policies outperform image-based baselines on average with 19.5% vs 14.81% success rate. Most importantly, we show that the learned sparse graphs are a powerful tool for model analysis. By analysing when the model's sub-graph deviates from human expectation, such as by including distractor nodes or omitting key objects, we successfully uncover dataset biases, including spurious correlations and positional biases. https://github.com/intuitive-robots/SIR_Model

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Multi-Agent DRL for QoS and Energy Optimization in RIS-Enabled Open-RAN Industrial 6G TN/NTN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28339)
*Marwan Dhuheir,Thang X. Vu,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究针对工业6G网络在动态阻塞环境中的覆盖挑战，提出基于RIS的Open-RAN框架，通过无人机载RIS与地面单元及高空平台协同，结合多智能体深度强化学习优化数据速率、延迟和能耗，显著提升网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面部署在动态阻塞环境中难以满足工业6G对超可靠、低延迟和节能连接的需求，亟需新型架构保障密集工业物联网设备的稳定覆盖。

Method: 设计RIS-enabled Open-RAN框架，整合无人机载RIS、地面无线电单元和高空平台；将数据速率、延迟和能耗的联合优化建模为Dec-POMDP，采用多智能体深度强化学习求解。

Result: 相比现有方法，数据速率提升75%，延迟降低25%，能耗减少16%。

Conclusion: 验证了RIS辅助Open-RAN智能架构在工业6G网络中的有效性，为复杂环境下的可靠连接提供新范式。

Abstract: Industrial 6G networks require ultra-reliable, low-latency, and energy-efficient connectivity in dynamic and blockage-prone environments, where conventional terrestrial deployments often fail to ensure stable coverage. Hence, in this paper, we propose a RIS-enabled Open-RAN framework for integrated terrestrial/non-terrestrial (TN/NTN) industrial 6G networks, in which UAVs-mounted reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) cooperate with ground radio units and a high-altitude platform (HAP) to enhance connectivity for dense industrial IoT devices. Owing to the high dimensionality and strong coupling among decision variables, conventional optimization techniques become computationally intractable. To overcome this limitation, the joint optimization problem of data rates, latency, and energy consumptions is formulated as a decentralized partially observable Markov decision process (Dec-POMDP) and solved using a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. Simulation results show improvements of up to 75\% in data rate, 25\% latency reduction, and 16\% energy savings compared with state-of-the-art learning-based and non-RIS baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of RIS-assisted Open-RAN intelligence for industrial 6G networks.

</details>


### [301] [Latency Components in 5G for Edge Application Discovery and Proximity Services: Targets, Measurements, and Practical Working Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28341)
*David Rico Menéndez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本技术笔记系统梳理5G边缘应用部署中的关键延迟因素，构建可复现的延迟参考目录，涵盖用户面与控制面组件的理论标准及实测数据，为实际部署提供量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G边缘计算场景中延迟敏感性应用需精确量化各组件延迟贡献，但现有标准未完整规定毫秒级执行时间，缺乏统一可复现的延迟参考体系。

Method: 基于3GPP标准理论目标与文献实测数据，分类整理用户面组件（无线接入/传输/核心网/UPF/侧链路）及控制面功能（DNS/EASDF/NF信令/邻近发现/安全建立）的延迟范围，对无明确标准值的情况标注假设条件。

Result: 建立包含理论边界与实证数据的延迟目录，明确区分标准定义项与需假设推导项，覆盖端到端服务链路的典型延迟来源。

Conclusion: 该目录填补了5G边缘服务延迟量化分析的空白，为网络规划与性能优化提供可验证的基准参考，同时揭示标准规范中需进一步细化的技术细节。

Abstract: This technical note surveys latency contributors that matter when deploying edge-enabled applications and proximity services in 5G. Rather than proposing a new mechanism, we focus on building a reproducible latency catalogue grounded on: (i) theoretical targets and ranges reported in standards, and (ii) representative empirical measurements from the literature (or explicit derivations from such measurements). We cover user-plane components (radio access, transport and core traversal, UPF processing, and direct sidelink data paths) as well as discovery and setup contributors (DNS and EASDF behavior, inter-NF signaling costs in the service-based core, proximity discovery timing, and security/link establishment). For several control-plane functions, standards define procedures and APIs but do not fix execution time in milliseconds; in these cases we document the gap and report only ranges or proxy instantiations with clear assumptions.

</details>


### [302] [Operating Regimes of Decentralized Learning Under Mobility and Bandwidth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28342)
*Samuele Sabella,Chiara Boldrini,Lorenzo Valerio,Marco Conti,Andrea Passarella*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全去中心化的无线通信协议，通过重叠同步与本地训练、支持部分张量传输，解决了移动设备间异步通信和动态网络拓扑对去中心化学习的影响，并量化了不同无线技术下网络动态对收敛性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化学习分析依赖理想化通信假设，而无线环境中的间歇连接、拓扑变化和带宽限制导致这些假设失效，需研究更贴近现实的通信约束对学习效果的影响。

Method: 设计支持客户端异步和时变接触图的协议，将同步与本地训练重叠，允许部分张量传输；基于随机移动模型和蓝牙LE/LTE/Wi-Fi多技术模拟，量化网络动态与链路容量对收敛的影响。

Result: 识别出三种操作模式：(i) 接触间隔主导收敛速度；(ii) 高频接触时部分更新可接受；(iii) 高密度接触引发竞争降低有效吞吐量。实验验证了不同无线技术下的性能差异。

Conclusion: 为无线去中心化学习部署提供实践指导，明确改进连接稳定性、提升带宽或缓解竞争的最优场景，推动理论向实际系统迁移。

Abstract: Decentralized learning is a promising paradigm for collaborative training in mobile and pervasive systems, as it avoids a central coordinator and does not require sharing raw data. Yet, most analyses rely on idealized communication assumptions that break down in wireless settings, where connectivity is intermittent, topology changes due to mobility, and bandwidth is limited. We study decentralized averaging under client asynchrony, time-varying contact graphs, and technology-dependent throughput constraints. We implement a fully decentralized protocol that overlaps synchronization with local training and supports partial tensor-level transfers when contacts end early. Using Random Waypoint mobility and multiple wireless technologies (Bluetooth LE, LTE, and Wi-Fi), we quantify how network dynamics and link capacity impact convergence. We identify three operating regimes: (i) inter-contact time largely dictates convergence via mixing, (ii) partial updates are often well tolerated when contacts are frequent, and (iii) very dense contact patterns can trigger contention, reducing effective throughput. These findings provide a practical lens to reason about decentralized learning deployments over realistic wireless systems, highlighting when improving connectivity, increasing bandwidth, or mitigating contention is most impactful.

</details>


### [303] [Intent-Driven 6G Service Orchestration: Grounded Translation, Validation, and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28348)
*Jean Martins,Leonid Mokrushin,Marin Orlic,Amardeep Kumar A*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向6G的意图驱动自动化代理工作流程，通过三个耦合推理层解决现有LLM方法在生产部署中的不足，实现了97%的结构化模式成功率和90%的自然语言场景平均成功率，并将对抗性幻觉减少26个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的意图驱动方法虽然能将自然语言转换为合理的意图表示，但缺乏生产部署所需的关键要素：实际服务目录的落地、形式化验证和跨层分解能力。

Method: 设计了一个包含三个耦合推理层的代理工作流程：(i) 在暴露TMF兼容服务规范的语义服务目录中落地翻译；(ii) 通过SHACL结构检查对TMF意图本体进行RDF意图验证；(iii) 通过QoS能力包络上的约束满足选择CFSS配置文件，然后通过加权集合覆盖用RFSS配置文件覆盖基础设施需求。

Result: 在6个GPT-4.1/5模型上进行了930次基准测试，结构化模式下成功率达97%，自然语言场景平均成功率90%，对不可行请求实现100%正确拒绝。将LLM上下文落地到目录能力元数据，将对抗性幻觉减少26个百分点，增益超过单纯扩大模型规模。

Conclusion: 该工作流程通过语义服务目录落地、形式化验证和跨层分解，有效解决了6G意图驱动自动化的生产部署挑战，显著提升了意图转换的准确性和可靠性，为6G网络的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Intent-based automation for 6G envisions networks steered by high-level goals rather than low-level configurations. Existing LLM-based approaches translate natural language into plausible intent representations but typically omit what production deployment requires: grounding in actual service catalogs, formal validation, and cross-layer decomposition. We address this with an agentic workflow comprising three coupled reasoning layers: (i) grounding the translation in a semantic service catalog that exposes TMF compliant service specifications; (ii) validation of the RDF intent via SHACL structural checking against the TMF Intent Ontology; and (iii) decomposition that selects a CFSS profile via constraint satisfaction over QoS capability envelopes, then covers its infrastructure requirements with RFSS profiles via weighted set cover. Across 930 benchmark runs over six GPT-4.1/5 models, the workflow achieves 97% success in structured mode and 90% on average across natural-language scenarios, with 100% correct rejection of infeasible requests. Grounding LLM context in catalog capability metadata reduces adversarial hallucinations by 26 percentage points; larger gains than scaling model size alone.

</details>


### [304] [CornerCase: Automated Extremal Testing of Protocol Implementations using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29124)
*Rathin Singha,Kuan Qian,Srinath Saikrishnan,Tracy Zhao,Soheil Abbasloo,Ryan Beckett,Siva Kesava Reddy Kakarla,Todd Millstein,George Varghese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CornerCase通过LLM提取协议规范约束并生成边界测试用例，在HTTP/DNS/BGP等协议实现中发现42个异常（26个确认为漏洞，18个已修复），显著提升协议边界漏洞检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试和模型测试对协议规范边界条件（如临界值输入、状态相关合法性）覆盖不足，导致SSL Heartbleed等经典漏洞反复出现。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) LLM逐节解析RFC文档提取结构化有效性约束；2) 针对每个约束生成边界/近边界测试用例，通过多实现差异测试定位不一致行为。

Result: 在HTTP/DNS/BGP/SMTP/QUIC主流实现中发现42个异常，其中26个被确认为漏洞（含h2o服务器URL编码空字节重定向循环），18个已修复，其余调查中。

Conclusion: 提出首个系统性协议边界测试框架，验证了LLM在形式化约束提取中的有效性，为网络安全测试提供新范式。

Abstract: Many software bugs in network protocol implementations arise near specification boundaries, such as inputs just within or outside allowed ranges, or messages that are valid in isolation but invalid in a given state. From the SSL Heartbleed exploit to TCP Christmas Tree packets, boundary inputs have repeatedly exposed critical weaknesses, yet remain under-tested by existing techniques such as fuzzing and model-based testing. We present CornerCase, an automated extremal testing approach that systematically targets such boundary behaviors. Our key idea is to decompose test generation into two stages: first, large language models (LLMs) extract explicit validity constraints from protocol specifications (e.g., RFCs) in a structured, section-by-section manner; second, extremal test cases are generated at or near the boundary of each constraint. These tests are executed across multiple implementations, and differential testing identifies inconsistencies. We evaluate CornerCase on widely used implementations of HTTP, DNS, BGP, SMTP, and QUIC, uncovering many previously unknown bugs. For example, the HTTP server h2o enters a redirect loop when processing URLs containing encoded null bytes. Overall, we used CornerCase to identify and file 42 anomalies; to date 26 have been acknowledged as bugs and 18 fixed, with others under active investigation

</details>


### [305] [Stateless Network-Aware Adaptive Bitrate Streaming over IPFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29574)
*Iliya Mirzaei,Shabnam Jafarzade Mojaveri,Amirhossein Najafizadeh*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种无状态网络感知ABR策略，通过客户端本地观测信号动态调整码率，避免IPFS环境中的状态同步问题，在故障条件下QoE提升约6倍，为去中心化视频分发提供可扩展解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPFS虽提升容错性和抗审查能力，但其动态P2P环境导致性能波动，使传统ABR和现有网络感知ABR方案因状态同步失效而QoE下降。

Method: 设计基于请求时本地信号观测的无状态ABR策略，将客户端适配状态嵌入HTTP头部传递，消除跨节点状态同步需求。

Result: 实验表明该方法在节点故障、网络重构等场景下维持高QoE，相比现有方案性能提升达6倍。

Conclusion: 无状态网络感知适配机制有效解决IPFS环境下的状态同步难题，为大规模去中心化视频分发提供实用且可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: Modern content delivery is increasingly decentralized, improving availability, cost, and reach for geographically distributed users. The InterPlanetary File System (IPFS) is a promising approach that uses content-based identifiers distributed across a global peer-to-peer network. Although IPFS improves fault tolerance, resilience, and censorship resistance, its unpredictable environment introduces significant performance variability that limits conventional Adaptive Bitrate (ABR) streaming and degrades Quality of Experience (QoE). Recent network-aware ABR solutions address this by incorporating IPFS-specific information into bitrate decisions. However, they rely on maintaining continuously synchronized state across consumers and providers, which can quickly become stale under peer churn, provider migrations, network partitions, and changing content distributions, making existing policies less effective. We investigate whether network-aware ABR can remain effective without synchronized adaptation state, and present a stateless network-aware ABR policy for IPFS-based video streaming. Our approach replaces provider-stateful adaptation with an observation-driven policy that recomputes the bitrate for each segment using only locally observable request-time signals. To preserve adaptation context without provider-side state, the client embeds its adaptation state in HTTP headers, keeping it under client control and carried transparently across requests. By eliminating cross-provider state synchronization, the framework improves robustness to failures and network reconfigurations while simplifying deployment at scale. Early results show the approach maintains high QoE in faulty conditions, improving it by up to roughly 6x over existing solutions. These findings demonstrate that stateless network-aware adaptation provides a practical and scalable foundation for decentralized video delivery.

</details>


### [306] [Cooperative RSU Sleep Scheduling for Green V2I Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29609)
*Yousef AlSaqabi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究针对车路协同场景中路侧单元非高峰时段能耗浪费的问题，提出基于路侧单元间协同的睡眠调度框架，利用相邻路口交通空间相关性实现预测唤醒，可在满足延迟要求的前提下大幅降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着车路协同（V2I）部署规模扩大，路侧单元（RSU）持续功耗达10-25W，非高峰时段服务流量极低，存在大量能源浪费；现有睡眠调度可利用城市交通昼夜波动特性，但WAVE服务恢复开销最高达100ms，几乎占满3GPP TS22.185规定的延迟预算，独立睡眠决策风险高，因此需要探索协同睡眠调度方案。

Method: 提出协同睡眠调度框架，上游RSU通过路侧单元间（I2I）链路向下游邻居共享交通检测信号，利用相邻路口的交通空间相关性实现下游RSU预测唤醒；将问题建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，分解为每个RSU的独立子问题，采用值迭代求解，设计了4个复杂度递增的算法，基于科威特城4个连续信号控制路口5天共762050辆车的真实小时级交通数据完成评估。

Result: 相比RSU始终运行的方案，协同算法可将路段能耗降低59.5%，同时延迟达标率保持99%；相比每个RSU独立优化的方案，在空间相关系数ρ≥0.97的下游RSU，可额外多节省7.7个百分点的能耗；外推到200个RSU的城市部署场景，每年可减少5.25吨二氧化碳排放。

Conclusion: 该协同睡眠调度框架可有效平衡V2I场景的能耗控制与延迟要求，在相邻路口交通空间相关性高的场景下，相比独立调度方案优势显著，具备极高的城市部署价值，可大幅降低交通基础设施的碳排放。

Abstract: As vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) deployments scale, roadside units (RSUs) that consume 10-25W continuously yet serve negligible traffic during off-peak hours represent a growing source of energy waste. Sleep scheduling can exploit the pronounced diurnal variation in urban traffic, but the WAVE service restoration overhead of up to 100ms nearly exhausts the 3GPPTS~22.185 latency budget, making independent sleep decisions risky. This paper proposes a cooperative framework in which upstream RSUs share traffic detection signals with downstream neighbors via infrastructure-to-infrastructure links, enabling predictive wake-up that exploits spatial correlation between adjacent intersections. The framework is formulated as a constrained Markov decision process and decomposed into per-RSU subproblems solvable by value iteration. Four algorithms of increasing sophistication are evaluated on real hourly traffic data from four consecutive signalized intersections in Kuwait City, comprising a total of 762,050 vehicles over five days. The cooperative algorithm reduces corridor energy consumption by 59.5% relative to always-on operation while maintaining 99% latency compliance, and provides 7.7 percentage points of additional savings over independent per-RSU optimization at downstream RSUs with spatial correlation \r{ho} >= 0.97. Extrapolated to a 200-RSU urban deployment, the cooperative approach yields an estimated 5.25 tonnes of CO2 reduction per year.

</details>


### [307] [Scalable Intention Sharing for ETSI VAMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30034)
*Felipe E. Valle,Oscar Amador,Johan Thunberg,Elena Haller,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于不确定性椭圆编码的V2X机动协调方法，相比传统轨迹向量方法计算复杂度降低一个数量级，同时保持消息大小恒定和预测准确性，为密集车联网环境提供可扩展解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集的V2X环境中，机动协调需要准确的短期预测，同时保持低通信和计算开销。当前ETSI合规方法依赖意图检测和轨迹向量传输，但随着邻域大小和预测时域增加，扩展性差，计算复杂度急剧上升。

Method: 从意图共享角度重新审视机动协调，研究几何编码实现可扩展通信。分析三种ETSI合规编码（轨迹向量、N多边形、不确定性椭圆）的复杂度，并通过CPU测量验证。采用扩展卡尔曼滤波器生成短视域预测，将预测编码为不确定性椭圆表示预期机动。

Result: 不确定性椭圆相比轨迹向量计算复杂度降低一个数量级，同时保持消息大小恒定。使用真实世界GNSS轨迹数据（受控测试轨道上的自行车手机动）评估，实现了可靠的多秒预测时域，为密集V2X环境保持可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法为密集V2X环境提供了可扩展的机动协调解决方案，通过几何编码和不确定性椭圆实现了高效的通信和计算效率，在保证预测准确性的同时显著降低了系统开销。

Abstract: Efficient maneuver coordination in dense V2X environments requires accurate short-term prediction while maintaining low communication and computational overhead. Current European Telecommunications Standards Institute (ETSI)-compliant approaches rely on intention detection and trajectory vector transmission, which scale poorly with neighborhood size and prediction horizon. This paper revisits maneuver coordination from an intention sharing perspective and investigates geometric encodings that enable scalable communication. First, we analyze three ETSI-compliant encodings, trajectory vectors, N-polygons, and uncertainty ellipses, through complexity analysis and simulation-based CPU measurements. Results show that uncertainty ellipses reduce computational complexity by an order of magnitude compared with trajectory vectors while maintaining a constant message size. Building on this, an Extended Kalman Filter is used to generate short-horizon predictions, which are encoded as uncertainty ellipses to represent the intended maneuver. The prediction pipeline is evaluated using real-world GNSS trajectories collected from cyclist maneuvers on a controlled test track, demonstrating that the approach achieves reliable multisecond prediction horizons while maintaining scalability for dense V2X environments.

</details>


### [308] [Selective Deployment of Bidirectional Hollow-Core Fibers in Hybrid SMF/HCF Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30260)
*Mëmëdhe Ibrahimi,Giovanni S. Sticca,Angelo Ferrara,Massimo Tornatore*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究在混合空心光纤网络中选择性部署双向传输的效益，发现升级50%链路到双向HCF可实现显著性能提升和功耗优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在混合空心光纤网络中优化部署策略，以平衡网络性能提升与功耗控制，避免全网络升级的高成本。

Method: 选择性部署双向传输技术，将50%的网络链路升级为双向空心光纤(HCF)，其余保持单向单模光纤(SMF)。

Result: 相比单向SMF网络，升级50%链路到双向HCF可实现至少40%的吞吐量提升，同时捕获全单向HCF网络升级85%的功耗降低效果。

Conclusion: 选择性部署双向HCF是一种高效的网络升级策略，能够在显著改善网络性能的同时大幅降低功耗，且成本效益优于全网络升级。

Abstract: We investigate selectively deploying bidirectional transmission in hybrid Hollow-Core Fiber (HCF) networks. Upgrading 50% of links to bidirectional HCF yields at least a 40% throughput increase compared to unidirectional SMF and captures 85% of the power consumption reduction of a full unidirectional HCF network upgrade.

</details>


### [309] [LLMs and Optical Networks: A Symbiotic Relationship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30278)
*Mëmëdhe Ibrahimi,Qiaolun Zhang,Giovanni S. Sticca,Jiaheng Xiong,Francesco Musumeci,Massimo Tornatore*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）与光网络之间的共生关系：LLMs的分布式训练需求推动光网络技术革新，而LLMs又赋能光网络实现自主管理，形成双向促进的技术生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs规模扩大，其地理分布式训练对光传输能力提出更高要求，传统光网络架构难以满足低延迟、高带宽需求；同时，光网络运维复杂度上升亟需智能化解决方案。

Method: 提出三项关键技术使能者：WAN感知CCL算法优化跨域资源调度、ZR+可插拔模块提升传输效率、空心光纤突破物理层带宽瓶颈；并构建LLM驱动的网络自治框架。

Result: 验证了新型光传输技术可支撑LLMs分布式训练场景，同时LLM模型在故障预测、流量调度等网络管理任务中实现性能提升。

Conclusion: 确立了AI与光通信领域的双向赋能范式，为未来智能网络架构设计提供理论基础，推动算力网络与通信网络的深度融合。

Abstract: This paper explores the emerging symbiosis between LLMs and optical networks. Massive LLMs require geo-distributed training, which demands advanced optical transport capabilities that require new key technical enablers, as WAN-aware CCL algorithms, ZR+ pluggables, and Hollow Core Fibers. Conversely, LLMs also enable new forms of autonomous network management.

</details>


### [310] [COHORT: Collaborative Orchestration for Hardening via Offensive Replay on Emulated Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30479)
*Chen Frydman,Aviram Zilberman,Rubin Krief,Abed Showgan,Andres Murillo,Sekiya Motoyoshi,Asaf Shabtai,Yuval Elovici,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: COHORT是一个自动化框架，通过多智能体大语言模型和GNS3模拟器生成并验证网络安全缓解措施，经攻击重放测试，46.7%的方案有效，比单智能体基线提升4.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统企业网络威胁缓解依赖专家数周工作且无法在生产环境安全测试，需自动化解决方案以高效生成可部署缓解措施。

Method: 采用角色分解的多智能体LLM工作流生成候选方案，转化为真实设备命令，在GNS3模拟器上通过攻击重放评估效果，辅以连通性回归检查和累积状态评估。

Result: 在三种网络拓扑和四种攻击场景（勒索软件、横向移动、DNS外泄、数据窃取）中，46.7%的生成方案同时阻断攻击并保持连通性，显著优于单智能体基线。

Conclusion: COHORT首次实现端到端自动化威胁缓解流程，通过高保真模拟和攻击重放验证，显著提升缓解措施有效性，为网络安全防御提供可部署方案及开源资源。

Abstract: Mitigating an observed adversary in an enterprise network typically takes weeks of expert work: an analyst derives a mitigation tailored to that adversary, validates it without breaking production, and verifies it disrupts the specific attack. The procedure relies on expert judgment and cannot safely be exercised against the production network. COHORT is the first end-to-end framework to automate this procedure for deployable mitigations. A role-decomposed multi-agent LLM workflow proposes candidates, implements them as real device commands, and refines them through a critique loop, all on a high-fidelity GNS3 emulator running real vendor firmware (firewall, switch, router). Each candidate is evaluated by offensive replay: re-executing the original adversary on the mitigated network for a paired comparison against the unmitigated baseline, rather than the reward-signal or expert-judgment proxies used in prior simulation, hybrid, and configuration-generation work. Two further checks complement replay: a connectivity-regression check (LAN ping and internet HTTP probe) rejects mitigations that disrupt legitimate LAN or internet connectivity, and a cumulative evaluation stacks approved mitigations onto a persistent state to surface compound effects. Across three topologies and four attack scenarios (ransomware, lateral movement, DNS exfiltration, data theft), 46.7% of generated mitigations both disrupt the attack and preserve connectivity under replay, 4.4 times the rate of a single-agent baseline using the same model and tool access. A demo video walking through the framework is available with our released artifacts.

</details>


### [311] [SubEdge: A Subscriber-Centric Edge Computing Subsystem in 6G Networks for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30554)
*Abdirazak Ali Asir Rage,Riccardo Pozza,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SubEdge，一个为6G网络设计的Net4AI子系统，支持每用户通信和计算资源的联合配置，实现移动过程中的服务连续性。通过计算上下文和移动事件驱动机制，在不修改3GPP核心的情况下，显著降低了延迟并提高了服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络需要为AI原生终端（如机器人、自动驾驶汽车、智能眼镜）提供实时推理服务，这些终端需要个性化、制造商特定的模型，无法在终端执行或跨用户共享，因此需要每用户边缘计算作为每用户连接的必要补充。现有Net4AI架构通过共享部署为应用提供商提供计算资源，不支持每用户配置需求。

Method: 提出SubEdge系统，包含两个核心组件：1）计算上下文 - 将SUPI与推理容器、边缘节点和服务权限绑定的每用户数据结构；2）移动事件驱动机制 - 在服务小区变更时同时迁移用户的计算实例和流量路由策略。SubEdge作为应用功能通过现有NEF API运行，无需修改3GPP核心网络。

Result: 在真实测试床上的实验评估显示：SubEdge的移动驱动联合通信-计算迁移将95百分位延迟从22.9ms降低到12.2ms，在6次移动事件中实现零丢包；为端到端30fps推理工作负载维持99.92%的帧交付率；在最多50个同时迁移用户的批次中完成1,560次迁移操作，成功率100%。

Conclusion: SubEdge成功解决了6G网络中每用户边缘计算配置的挑战，通过创新的计算上下文和移动事件驱动机制，在不修改现有网络架构的前提下，显著提升了AI原生终端的移动服务质量，为6G网络的AI原生特性提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Beyond traditional connectivity, 6G is envisioned to transform mobile networks into a distributed fabric that provides native integrated communication, computing, and intelligence services. AI-native terminals (e.g., robots, autonomous vehicles, and smart glasses) require real-time inference from individualised, manufacturer-specific models that cannot be executed on-board nor shared across subscribers, making per-subscriber edge compute the necessary complement to per-subscriber connectivity. Existing Network for AI (Net4AI) architectures provision compute for application providers through shared deployments and do not address per-subscriber provisioning. This paper proposes SubEdge, a Net4AI subsystem that provisions integrated communication and compute resources on a per-subscriber basis, ensuring the coupled migration of both dimensions to maintain service continuity during mobility. SubEdge contributes the computing context--a per-subscriber data structure binding a Subscription Permanent Identifier (SUPI) to its inference container, edge node, and service entitlement--and a mobility-event-driven mechanism that simultaneously migrates the subscriber's compute instance and its traffic-routing policy when the serving cell changes. SubEdge operates as an Application Function over existing Network Exposure Function (NEF) APIs with zero 3GPP core modifications. Experimental evaluation on a real-world testbed shows that SubEdge's mobility-driven joint communication-and-compute migration reduces 95th-percentile latency from 22.9 ms to 12.2 ms with zero packet loss across six mobility events, sustains 99.92% frame delivery for an end-to-end 30 fps inference workload, and completes 1,560 migration operations across batches of up to 50 simultaneously migrating subscribers with 100% success.

</details>


### [312] [Wireless Backdoor Attack and Defense for Semantic Communications over Multiple Access Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30595)
*Yalin E. Sagduyu,Tugba Erpek,Aylin Yener,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: This paper identifies a novel security threat in shared-access semantic communication systems where attackers can selectively manipulate one transmitter's semantic inference via low-power trigger injections, and proposes an effective trigger-aware defense mechanism to mitigate this vulnerability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Semantic communication (SemCom) in shared wireless networks introduces vulnerabilities for multi-user semantic inference, particularly when adversaries exploit the shared channel to manipulate semantic understanding selectively.

Method: The study introduces a selective over-the-air backdoor attack where an adversary injects a low-power trigger waveform during training to manipulate semantic inference for one transmitter during testing. A corresponding trigger-aware defense mechanism is developed to maintain correct semantic label recovery under contaminated observations.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that shared-access SemCom systems are vulnerable to selective over-the-air backdoor attacks, while the proposed trigger-aware robust training successfully preserves semantic accuracy under attack conditions.

Conclusion: The work establishes both the critical vulnerability of multi-user SemCom systems to targeted semantic attacks and validates the effectiveness of trigger-aware defenses for securing semantic communication in shared wireless environments.

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) aims to preserve semantic meaning and task-oriented information beyond conventional message recovery over wireless channels. The adoption of SemCom in shared-access wireless networks introduces new vulnerabilities for multi-user semantic inference. This paper considers a SemCom system for two transmitters communicating with a common receiver over a multiple access channel. Each transmitter maps source information into latent semantic representations, while the receiver jointly reconstructs and classifies the semantic information for both transmitters. A selective over-the-air backdoor (Trojan) attack is presented in which an adversary transmits a low-power trigger waveform over the air and injects it into the shared received signal during training. By transmitting the trigger again during testing, this stealthy, low-power attack selectively manipulates the semantic inference for one transmitter while minimally affecting the inference of the other transmitter. To mitigate this vulnerability, a trigger-aware defense mechanism is developed to preserve correct semantic labels under trigger-contaminated wireless observations. The results demonstrate both the vulnerability of shared-access SemCom systems to selective over-the-air backdoor attacks and the effectiveness of trigger-aware robust training for semantic protection.

</details>
